A PSPNet ResNet-18 student closely approaches its ResNet-101 teacher despite using only one quarter of the parameters, reaching 99\% of the teacher's mIoU on Cityscapes (79.0 vs.\ 79.8) and 92\% on ADE20K. Our results challenge the prevailing assumption that KD for segmentation requires task-specific mechanisms and suggest that scaling, rather than complex hand-crafted objectives, should guide future method design.

</details>


### [73] [Beyond Fidelity: Semantic Similarity Assessment in Low-Level Image Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25408)
*Runjie Wang,Weiling Chen,Tiesong Zhao,Chang Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义相似度作为低级图像处理的新评估任务，并提出了基于三元组的语义相似度分数（T3S），通过前景实体、背景实体和关系建模图像语义，实验证明其优于现有保真度导向和语义级基线指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习和生成模型的发展，处理后的图像可能保持感知质量但改变语义内容，传统图像质量评估（IQA）不足以进行语义级评估，因此需要形式化语义相似度作为新的评估任务。

Method: 提出三元组语义相似度分数（T3S），通过前景实体、背景实体和关系三个维度建模图像语义，结合语义实体提取、前景-背景解耦以及开放世界类别/关系建模。

Result: 在COCO和SPA-Data数据集上，T3S一致优于现有保真度导向指标和代表性语义级基线，能更好反映多种退化下的渐进语义变化。

Conclusion: 语义评估在现代低级视觉中具有重要性，T3S为低级图像处理提供了有效的语义级评估指标。

Abstract: Low-level image processing has long been evaluated mainly from the perspective of visual fidelity. However, with the rise of deep learning and generative models, processed images may preserve perceptual quality while altering semantic content, making conventional Image Quality Assessment (IQA) insufficient for semantic-level assessment. In this paper, we formalize \textit{Semantic Similarity} as a new evaluation task for low-level image processing, aimed at measuring whether semantic content is preserved after processing. We further present a structured formulation of image semantics based on semantic entities and their relations, and discuss the desired properties and constraints of a valid semantic similarity index. Based on this formulation, we propose Triplet-based Semantic Similarity Score (T3S), which models image semantics through foreground entities, background entities, and relations. T3S combines semantic entity extraction, foreground-background disentanglement, and open-world class/relation modeling. Experiments on COCO and SPA-Data show that T3S consistently outperforms existing fidelity-oriented metrics and representative semantic-level baselines, while better reflecting progressive semantic changes under diverse degradations. These results highlight the importance of semantic assessment in modern low-level vision.

</details>


### [74] [A Systematic Post-Train Framework for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25427)
*Zeyue Xue,Siming Fu,Jie Huang,Shuai Lu,Haoran Li,Yijun Liu,Yuming Li,Xiaoxuan He,Mengzhao Chen,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向视频扩散模型的后训练框架，通过监督微调、基于GRPO的RLHF、提示增强和推理优化四个协同阶段，系统性地弥合预训练模型与真实部署之间的差距，显著提升视觉质量、时间一致性和指令遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视频扩散模型在预训练后存在提示敏感性、时间不一致性和高昂推理成本等关键问题，导致其与真实部署需求之间存在显著差距。

Method: 提出四阶段后训练框架：(1) 监督微调将基础模型转化为稳定的指令遵循策略；(2) 基于GRPO的RLHF阶段提升感知质量和时间连贯性；(3) 专用语言模型增强提示；(4) 推理优化提升系统效率。

Result: 实验表明该统一流程有效缓解常见伪影，显著改善可控性和视觉美学，同时严格遵守采样成本约束。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建可扩展、稳定、适应性强且实际有效的后训练流程提供了实践蓝图。

Abstract: While large-scale video diffusion models have demonstrated impressive capabilities in generating high-resolution and semantically rich content, a significant gap remains between their pretraining performance and real-world deployment requirements due to critical issues such as prompt sensitivity, temporal inconsistency, and prohibitive inference costs. To bridge this gap, we propose a comprehensive post-training framework that systematically aligns pretrained models with user intentions through four synergistic stages: we first employ Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) to transform the base model into a stable instruction-following policy, followed by a Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) stage that utilizes a novel Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) method tailored for video diffusion to enhance perceptual quality and temporal coherence; subsequently, we integrate Prompt Enhancement via a specialized language model to refine user inputs, and finally address system efficiency through Inference Optimization. Together, these components provide a systematic approach to improving visual quality, temporal coherence, and instruction following, while preserving the controllability learned during pretraining. The result is a practical blueprint for building scalable post-training pipelines that are stable, adaptable, and effective in real-world deployment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that this unified pipeline effectively mitigates common artifacts and significantly improves controllability and visual aesthetics while adhering to strict sampling cost constraints.

</details>


### [75] [SARU: A Shadow-Aware and Removal Unified Framework for Remote Sensing Images with New Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25432)
*Zi-Yang Bo,Wei Lu,Hongruixuan Chen,Si-Bao Chen,Bin Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SARU框架，统一处理遥感影像中的阴影检测与去除任务。通过双分支检测模块(DBCSF-Net)生成高保真阴影掩膜，再结合无训练物理算法(N²SGSR)从单张图像中恢复光照，无需成对训练数据。同时贡献了两个新基准数据集RSISD和SiSRB，实验表明SARU在多个数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感影像阴影处理方法存在两大问题：(1)将阴影检测与去除作为分离的级联任务，导致流程繁琐且误差累积；(2)多数深度学习方法依赖难以获取的成对阴影/非阴影图像进行训练。亟需一种无需成对数据、统一检测与去除且避免误差传播的鲁棒框架。

Method: 提出SARU两阶段框架：(1)双分支检测模块DBCSF-Net融合多色彩空间与语义特征生成高保真阴影掩膜，有效区分阴影与暗色物体；(2)基于阴影掩膜，提出无训练物理算法N²SGSR，通过从输入单张图像中相邻非阴影区域转移属性来恢复光照。同时构建了RSISD和SiSRB两个新基准数据集。

Result: 在公开AISD数据集及新提出的RSISD、SiSRB基准上，SARU均达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: SARU通过整合阴影检测与去除缓解了误差传播，消除了对成对训练数据的依赖，为真实遥感影像分析建立了鲁棒且实用的框架。

Abstract: Shadows are a prevalent problem in remote sensing imagery (RSI), degrading visual quality and severely limiting the performance of downstream tasks like object detection and semantic segmentation. Most prior works treat shadow detection and removal as separate, cascaded tasks, which can lead to cumbersome process and error accumulation. Furthermore, many deep learning methods rely on paired shadow and non-shadow images for training, which are often unavailable in practice. To address these challenges, we propose Shadow-Aware and Removal Unified (SARU) Framework , a cohesive two-stage framework. First, its dual-branch detection module (DBCSF-Net) fuses multi-color space and semantic features to generate high-fidelity shadow masks, effectively distinguishing shadows from dark objects. Then, leveraging these masks, a novel, training-free physical algorithm (N$^2$SGSR) restores illumination by transferring properties from adjacent non-shadow regions within the single input image. To facilitate rigorous evaluation and foster future work, we also introduce two new benchmark datasets: the RSI Shadow Detection (RSISD) dataset and the Single-image Shadow Removal Benchmark (SiSRB). Extensive experiments demonstrate that SARU achieves state-of-the-art performance on both the public AISD dataset and our newly introduced benchmarks. By holistically integrating shadow detection and removal to mitigate error propagation and eliminating the dependency on paired training data, SARU establishes a robust, practical framework for real-world RSI analysis. The source code and datasets are publicly available at: https://github.com/AeroVILab-AHU/SARU-Framework.

</details>


### [76] [GramSR: Visual Feature Conditioning for Diffusion-Based Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25457)
*Fabio D'Oronzio,Federico Putamorsi,Leonardo Zini,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GramSR提出了一种基于单步扩散的单图像超分辨率框架，用DINOv3编码器提取的密集视觉特征替代文本条件，并通过三阶段LoRA架构（像素级、语义级、纹理级）分别处理退化去除、感知细节增强和纹理一致性，在标准SR基准上优于现有单步扩散方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的SR方法依赖文本条件（语义描述），但文本只能提供高层语义，缺乏空间对齐的视觉细节信息，导致抽象语义与视觉细节之间存在表征鸿沟，限制了恢复的真实性和保真度。

Method: GramSR采用单步扩散框架，用预训练DINOv3编码器从低分辨率输入提取密集视觉特征替代文本条件。提出三阶段LoRA架构：1）像素级LoRA模块，使用ℓ₂损失进行退化去除；2）语义级LoRA模块，使用LPIPS和CSD损失增强感知细节；3）纹理级LoRA模块，使用DINOv3特征的Gram矩阵损失强制特征相关性一致性。推理时通过独立引导尺度灵活控制各模块效果。

Result: 在标准SR基准上，GramSR在结构保真度和纹理真实性方面一致优于现有单步扩散方法。

Conclusion: 用密集视觉特征替代文本条件并结合三阶段LoRA训练，能有效弥合语义与视觉细节之间的表征鸿沟，实现更高质量的单图像超分辨率重建。

Abstract: Despite recent advances, single-image super-resolution (SR) remains challenging, especially in real-world scenarios with complex degradations. Diffusion-based SR methods, particularly those built on Stable Diffusion, leverage strong generative priors but commonly rely on text conditioning derived from semantic captioning. Such textual descriptions provide only high-level semantics and lack the spatially aligned visual information required for faithful restoration, leading to a representation gap between abstract semantics and spatially aligned visual details. To address this limitation, we propose GramSR, a one-step diffusion-based SR framework that replaces text conditioning with dense visual features extracted from the low-resolution input using a pre-trained DINOv3 encoder. GramSR adopts a three-stage LoRA architecture, where pixel-level, semantic-level, and texture-level LoRA modules are trained sequentially. The pixel-level module focuses on degradation removal using $\ell_2$ loss, the semantic-level module enhances perceptual details via LPIPS and CSD losses, and the texture-level module enforces feature correlation consistency through a Gram matrix loss computed from DINOv3 features. At inference, independent guidance scales enable flexible control over degradation removal, semantic enhancement, and texture preservation. Extensive experiments on standard SR benchmarks demonstrate that GramSR consistently outperforms existing one-step diffusion-based methods, achieving superior structural fidelity and texture realism. The code for this work is available at: https://github.com/aimagelab/GramSR.

</details>


### [77] [Image Compression with Bubble-Aware Frame Rate Adaptation for Energy-Efficient Video Capsule Endoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25464)
*Oliver Bause,Jörg Gammerdinger,Julia Werner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对视频胶囊内窥镜(VCE)的图像压缩流水线和气泡感知的动态帧率自适应策略，在RISC-V平台上实现了5.748倍压缩比(82.6%)、40.3 dB峰值信噪比，系统整体能耗降低20.58%，帧率自适应进一步降低高达40%的能耗，在不影响诊断质量的前提下有效延长设备续航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频胶囊内窥镜尺寸受限导致电池续航短，而图像采集与传输能耗高，两者之间存在矛盾，亟需在保持诊断图像质量的同时减少数据传输量。

Method: 提出图像压缩流水线降低传输数据量；利用压缩过程特征识别低诊断价值帧(主要由气泡导致)，无需额外图像分析；对低可见度帧实施气泡感知的动态帧率自适应策略。

Result: 压缩比5.748(82.6%)，PSNR 40.3 dB；系统整体能耗平均降低20.58%；气泡感知帧率自适应能耗降低高达40%。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持诊断图像质量的同时显著降低能耗，展示了提升VCE实用性的潜力。

Abstract: Video Capsule Endoscopy (VCE) is a promising method for improving the medical examination of the small intestine in the gastrointestinal tract. A key challenge is their limited size, resulting in a short battery lifetime which conflicts with high energy consumption for image capturing and transmission to an on-body device. Thus, we propose an image compression pipeline that substantially reduces the transmitted data while preserving diagnostic image quality. Furthermore, we exploit characteristics of the compression process to identify frames with low diagnostic value mainly caused by bubbles, without requiring additional image analysis. For low-visibility frames, a dynamic bubble-aware frame rate adaptation strategy reduces image acquisition and transmission during these phases while preserving sensitivity to potential anomalies. The proposed compression and frame rate adaptation are evaluated on a RISC-V platform using the Kvasir-Capsule and Galar datasets. The compression method achieves a compression ratio of 5.748 (82.6%) at a peak signal-to-noise ratio of 40.3 dB, indicating negligible loss of visual quality. The compression accomplished a mean energy reduction of the whole system by 20.58%. Additionally, the proposed bubble-aware frame rate adaptation reduced the energy consumption by up to 40%. These results demonstrate the potential of our method to increase the applicability of VCE.

</details>


### [78] [Prefill-Time Intervention for Mitigating Hallucination in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25642)
*Chengsheng Zhang,Chenghao Sun,Xinyan Jiang,Wei Li,Xinmei Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Prefill-Time Intervention (PTI)，一种在预填充阶段进行单次干预的新范式，通过模态感知的键值缓存修正来减轻LVLM的幻觉问题，避免解码阶段错误累积，并可与现有方法即插即用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于转向向量的方法仅在解码阶段干预，导致错误自回归累积，无意中放大了残余幻觉的严重性。作者希望从源头纠正幻觉倾向的表征。

Method: PTI在预填充阶段仅干预一次，增强初始KV缓存。具体地，PTI是模态感知的，为视觉和文本表征推导不同的方向；干预是解耦的，分别引导键（Key）朝向视觉锚定对象、值（Value）过滤背景噪声。

Result: 大量实验表明PTI在缓解幻觉方面效果显著，且具有跨解码策略、LVLM和基准的泛化能力。PTI与现有解码阶段方法正交，可即插即用提升性能。

Conclusion: PTI通过在错误累积前干预预填充阶段，有效缓解了LVLM的幻觉问题，是一种通用且可集成的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in visual-textual understanding, yet their reliability is critically undermined by hallucinations, i.e., the generation of factually incorrect or inconsistent responses. While recent studies using steering vectors demonstrated promise in reducing hallucinations, a notable challenge remains: they inadvertently amplify the severity of residual hallucinations. We attribute this to their exclusive focus on the decoding stage, where errors accumulate autoregressively and progressively worsen subsequent hallucinatory outputs. To address this, we propose Prefill-Time Intervention (PTI), a novel steering paradigm that intervenes only once during the prefill stage, enhancing the initial Key-Value (KV) cache before error accumulation occurs. Specifically, PTI is modality-aware, deriving distinct directions for visual and textual representations. This intervention is decoupled to steer keys toward visually-grounded objects and values to filter background noise, correcting hallucination-prone representations at their source. Extensive experiments demonstrate PTI's significant performance in mitigating hallucinations and its generalizability across diverse decoding strategies, LVLMs, and benchmarks. Moreover, PTI is orthogonal to existing decoding-stage methods, enabling plug-and-play integration and further boosting performance. Code is available at: https://github.com/huaiyi66/PTI.

</details>


### [79] [Generalizable Human Gaussian Splatting via Multi-view Semantic Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25466)
*Jingi Kim,Wonjun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于跨视图注意力机制的可泛化人体高斯泼溅方法，通过将多视图潜在特征反投影到共享3D空间并重新校准，解决了稀疏视图下人体复杂姿态导致的特征不一致和空间模糊问题，显著提升了渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可泛化人体高斯泼溅方法在处理稀疏多视图输入时，因人体关节复杂运动和多视图间重叠区域有限，导致特征表示不一致，从而影响3D高斯的精确定位和渲染质量。

Method: 将每个视角编码的潜在嵌入通过预测的深度图反投影到共享3D空间，并基于跨视图注意力机制对属于同一身体部位的特征进行重新校准，从而解决高纹理区域和遮挡部位的空间模糊性。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法有效提升了稀疏视图下可泛化人体高斯泼溅的渲染性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的跨视图特征反投影与重校准机制能够有效解决多视图特征不一致问题，实现更精确的3D高斯定位，最终提升人体渲染质量。

Abstract: Recently, generalizable human Gaussian splatting from sparse-view inputs has been actively studied for the photorealistic human rendering. Most existing methods rely on explicit geometric constraints or predefined structural representations to accurately position 3D Gaussians. Although these approaches have shown the remarkable progress in this field, they still suffer from inconsistent feature representations across multi-view inputs due to complex articulations of the human body and limited overlaps between different views. To address this problem, we propose a novel method to accurately localize 3D Gaussians and ultimately improve the quality of human rendering. The key idea is to unproject latent embeddings encoded from each viewpoint into a shared 3D space through predicted depth maps and recalibrate them belonging to the same body part based on cross-view attention. This helps the model resolve the spatial ambiguity occurring in highly textured regions as well as occluded body parts, thus leading to the accurate localization of 3D Gaussians. Experimental results on benchmark datasets show that the proposed method efficiently improves the performance of generalizable human Gaussian splatting from sparse-view inputs.

</details>


### [80] [DualGeo: A Dual-View Framework for Worldwide Image Geo-localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25533)
*Junchao Cui,Wenqi Shi,Shaoyong Du,Hang He,Xuanzi Ma,Hao Tang,Xiangyang Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DualGeo是一个两阶段全球图像地理定位框架，首先通过双向交叉注意力融合图像和语义分割特征，再通过双视图对比学习对齐GPS坐标构建检索数据库；然后通过地理聚类重排序候选结果，并利用大型多模态模型进行最终坐标预测。在IM2GPS、IM2GPS3k和YFCC4k数据集上，街道级和城市级定位精度分别提升3.6%-16.58%和1.29%-8.77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全球图像地理定位方法依赖对环境变化（光照、季节、天气等）敏感的视觉特征，且缺乏有效的后处理来过滤异常候选结果，限制了定位精度。

Method: DualGeo采用两阶段框架：第一阶段通过双向交叉注意力融合图像特征与语义分割特征，构建地理表征基础，再通过双视图对比学习与GPS坐标对齐建立全局检索数据库；第二阶段进行地理认知精炼，先通过地理聚类对检索候选进行重排序，再输入大型多模态模型（LMMs）进行最终坐标预测。

Result: 在IM2GPS、IM2GPS3k和YFCC4k数据集上，街道级（<1km）定位精度提升3.6%-16.58%，城市级（<25km）定位精度提升1.29%-8.77%，超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: DualGeo通过融合语义特征与地理聚类重排序，有效缓解了视觉特征对环境变化的敏感性及异常候选问题，显著提升了跨尺度的全球图像地理定位精度。

Abstract: Worldwide image geo-localization aims to infer the geographic location of an image captured anywhere on Earth, spanning street, city, regional, national, and continental scales. Existing methods rely on visual features that are sensitive to environmental variations (e.g., lighting, season, and weather) and lack effective post-processing to filter outlier candidates, limiting localization accuracy. To address these limitations, we propose DualGeo, a two-stage framework for worldwide image geo-localization. First, it establishes a geo-representational foundation by fusing image and semantic segmentation features via bidirectional cross-attention. The fused features are then aligned with GPS coordinates through dual-view contrastive learning to build a global retrieval database. Second, it performs geo-cognitive refinement by re-ranking retrieved candidates using geographic clustering. It then feeds them into large multimodal models (LMMs) for final coordinate prediction. Experiments on IM2GPS, IM2GPS3k, and YFCC4k show that DualGeo outperforms state-of-the-art methods, improving street-level (<1 km) and city-level (<25 km) localization accuracy by 3.6%-16.58% and 1.29%-8.77%, respectively. Our code and datasets are available : https://github.com/CJ310177/DualGeo.

</details>


### [81] [SIEVES: Selective Prediction Generalizes through Visual Evidence Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25855)
*Hector G. Rodriguez,Marcus Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SIEVES通过视觉证据评分实现选择性预测，在OOD场景下覆盖率提升高达3倍，且无需针对特定基准或推理器进行训练即可泛化到多种模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VQA基准趋于饱和，但在实际OOD场景中需要满足低容错率。现有选择性预测方法缺乏视觉定位证据，导致泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出SIEVES框架：1) 推理器生成答案时同时产生局部化视觉证据；2) 选择器显式学习评估推理器提供的定位质量，据此决定是否弃权。

Result: 在V* Bench、HR-Bench-8k、MME-RealWorld-Lite、VizWiz、AdVQA等OOD基准上，覆盖率提升高达3倍；可迁移至o3、Gemini-3-Pro等闭源推理器，且提升效果超越单纯准确率提升带来的收益。

Conclusion: SIEVES在5个OOD数据集和3种推理器上均表现出一致泛化能力，无需针对特定基准或推理器进行训练或适配，证明了基于视觉证据评分的选择性预测范式的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve ever-stronger performance on visual-language tasks. Even as traditional visual question answering benchmarks approach saturation, reliable deployment requires satisfying low error tolerances in real-world out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. Precisely, selective prediction aims to improve coverage, i.e. the share of inputs the system answers, while adhering to a user-defined risk level. This is typically achieved by assigning a confidence score to each answer and abstaining on those that fall below a certain threshold. To enable reliable generalization, we require reasoner models to produce localized visual evidence while answering, and design a selector that explicitly learns to estimate the quality of the localization provided by the reasoner. We show that SIEVES (Selective Prediction through Visual Evidence Scoring) improves coverage by up to three times on challenging OOD benchmarks (V* Bench, HR-Bench-8k, MME-RealWorld-Lite, VizWiz, and AdVQA), compared to non-grounding baselines. Beyond better generalization to OOD tasks, the design of the SIEVES selector enables transfer to proprietary reasoners without access to their weights or logits, such as o3 and Gemini-3-Pro, providing coverage boosts beyond those attributable to accuracy alone. We highlight that SIEVES generalizes across all five tested OOD datasets and reasoner models (Pixel-Reasoner, o3, and Gemini-3-Pro), without benchmark- or reasoner-specific training or adaptation.

</details>


### [82] [Vision SmolMamba: Spike-Guided Token Pruning for Energy-Efficient Spiking State-Space Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25570)
*Dewei Bai,Hongxiang Peng,Yunyun Zeng,Ziyu Zhang,Hong Qu,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Vision SmolMamba，一种将脉冲驱动动力学与线性时间选择性递归相结合的脉冲状态空间架构。核心是脉冲引导时空标记剪枝器(SST-TP)，通过脉冲激活强度和首脉冲延迟估计标记重要性，逐步移除冗余标记。该方法在ImageNet-1K、CIFAR等静态和事件基准上实现了优于脉冲Transformer和脉冲Mamba基线的精度-效率权衡，能量消耗至少降低1.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脉冲Transformer在长程视觉建模中表现良好，但其二次复杂度的标记交互与脉冲神经计算的稀疏、事件驱动本质不匹配，导致计算效率低下。

Method: 提出SST-TP（脉冲引导时空标记剪枝器），利用脉冲激活强度和首脉冲延迟估计标记重要性，逐步移除冗余标记；将脉冲事件直接集成到双向状态空间递归中形成SmolMamba模块，构建线性时间复杂度的脉冲状态空间视觉骨干。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K、CIFAR10/100、CIFAR10-DVS、DVS128 Gesture等基准上，Vision SmolMamba相比脉冲Transformer和脉冲Mamba基线，估计能耗至少降低1.5倍，同时保持竞争性或更优的准确率。

Conclusion: 脉冲引导的标记稀疏性与状态空间建模相结合，为脉冲视觉系统提供了一种可扩展且能量高效的范式。

Abstract: Spiking Transformers have shown strong potential for long-range visual modeling through spike-driven self-attention. However, their quadratic token interactions remain fundamentally misaligned with the sparse and event-driven nature of spiking neural computation. To address this limitation, we propose Vision SmolMamba, an energy-efficient spiking state-space architecture that integrates spike-driven dynamics with linear-time selective recurrence. The key idea is a Spike-Guided Spatio-Temporal Token Pruner (SST-TP), which estimates token importance using both spike activation strength and first-spike latency. This mechanism progressively removes redundant tokens while preserving salient spatio-temporal information, enabling efficient scaling with token sparsity. Based on this mechanism, the proposed SmolMamba block incorporates spike events directly into bidirectional state-space recurrence, forming a spiking state-space vision backbone for efficient long-range modeling. Extensive experiments on both static and event-based benchmarks, including ImageNet-1K, CIFAR10/100, CIFAR10-DVS, and DVS128 Gesture, demonstrate that Vision SmolMamba consistently achieves superior accuracy-efficiency trade-offs. In particular, it reduces the estimated energy cost by at least 1.5x compared with prior spiking Transformer baselines and a Spiking Mamba variant while maintaining competitive or improved accuracy. These results demonstrate that combining spike-guided token sparsity with state-space modeling offers a scalable and energy-efficient paradigm for spiking vision systems.

</details>


### [83] [Control Your Queries: Heterogeneous Query Interaction for Camera-Radar Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25574)
*Jialong Wu,Yihan Wang,Matthias Rottmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的异构查询交互融合范式ConFusion，通过图像查询、雷达查询和可学习世界查询的异构查询混合（QMix）与交互式查询交换采样（QSwap），在nuScenes数据集上实现了相机-雷达3D目标检测的SOTA性能（验证集59.1 mAP/65.6 NDS，测试集61.6 mAP/67.9 NDS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机-雷达融合在自动驾驶中具有互补感知和低成本的优势，但现有方法的融合方式（输入混合、特征图混合或基于查询的特征采样）仍存在不足，需探索更有效的异构查询交互机制。

Method: 1) 提出ConFusion检测器，组合图像查询、雷达查询和3D空间分布的可学习世界查询以改善初始化与覆盖；2) 提出异构查询混合（QMix），在特征采样后执行专用跨类型注意力以整合互补证据；3) 提出交互式查询交换采样（QSwap），允许相关查询在注意力与几何约束下交换信息特征token，优化特征采样。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，验证集达到59.1 mAP和65.6 NDS，测试集达到61.6 mAP和67.9 NDS，均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 异构查询交互范式能有效融合相机与雷达特征，优于现有融合方法，为多模态3D检测提供了新思路。

Abstract: In autonomous driving, camera-radar fusion offers complementary sensing and low deployment cost. Existing methods perform fusion through input mixing, feature map mixing, or query-based feature sampling. We propose a new fusion paradigm, termed heterogeneous query interaction, and present ConFusion, a camera-radar 3D object detector. ConFusion combines image queries, radar queries, and learnable world queries distributed in 3D space to improve query initialization and object coverage. To encourage cross-type interaction among heterogeneous queries, we introduce heterogeneous query mixing (QMix), which performs dedicated cross-type attention after feature sampling to consolidate complementary object evidence. We further propose interactive query swap sampling (QSwap), which improves feature sampling by allowing related queries to exchange informative feature tokens under attention and geometric constraints. Experiments on the nuScenes dataset show that ConFusion achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching 59.1 mAP and 65.6 NDS on the validation set, and 61.6 mAP and 67.9 NDS on the test set.

</details>


### [84] [No Pedestrian Left Behind: Real-Time Detection and Tracking of Vulnerable Road Users for Adaptive Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25887)
*Anas Gamal Aly,Hala ElAarag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出No Pedestrian Left Behind (NPLB)系统，一种实时自适应交通信号控制系统，通过集成YOLOv12目标检测与ByteTrack多目标跟踪，在行人过街时间不足时自动延长信号时长。在BGVP数据集上YOLOv12达到0.756 mAP@0.5，蒙特卡洛模拟表明该系统将弱势道路使用者滞留率从9.10%降至2.60%，安全性提升71.4%，且仅在12.1%的过街周期中触发信号延长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行人过街信号采用固定定时控制，无法根据行人行为调整，导致老年人、残障人士或分心行人等弱势道路使用者（VRU）在信号变换时被困于路中，存在安全隐患。

Method: NPLB系统包含三个核心组件：1) 基于BGVP数据集微调的YOLOv12目标检测模型（mAP@0.5达0.756）；2) ByteTrack多目标跟踪模块；3) 自适应控制器，在剩余通行时间低于临界阈值时自动延长行人相位。通过10,000次蒙特卡洛模拟评估系统性能。

Result: NPLB将VRU滞留率从9.10%降至2.60%，安全性提升71.4%，信号延长仅需在12.1%的过街周期中触发。YOLOv12在BGVP数据集上mAP@0.5达0.756，优于五种SOTA检测模型。

Conclusion: NPLB系统通过实时自适应信号控制有效减少弱势道路使用者的滞留风险，在仅需少量信号延长干预的条件下显著提升安全性，为智能交通系统中的行人保护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Current pedestrian crossing signals operate on fixed timing without adjustment to pedestrian behavior, which can leave vulnerable road users (VRUs) such as the elderly, disabled, or distracted pedestrians stranded when the light changes. We introduce No Pedestrian Left Behind (NPLB), a real-time adaptive traffic signal system that monitors VRUs in crosswalks and automatically extends signal timing when needed. We evaluated five state-of-the-art object detection models on the BGVP dataset, with YOLOv12 achieving the highest mean Average Precision at 50% (mAP@0.5) of 0.756. NPLB integrates our fine-tuned YOLOv12 with ByteTrack multi-object tracking and an adaptive controller that extends pedestrian phases when remaining time falls below a critical threshold. Through 10,000 Monte Carlo simulations, we demonstrate that NPLB improves VRU safety by 71.4%, reducing stranding rates from 9.10% to 2.60%, while requiring signal extensions in only 12.1% of crossing cycles.

</details>


### [85] [Refinement via Regeneration: Enlarging Modification Space Boosts Image Refinement in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25636)
*Jiayi Guo,Linqing Wang,Jiangshan Wang,Yang Yue,Zeyu Liu,Zhiyuan Zhao,Qinglin Lu,Gao Huang,Chunyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Refinement via Regeneration (RvR)框架，将文本到图像生成中的精炼过程从编辑范式（RvE）重构为条件图像再生范式，通过利用初始图像的语义token进行条件生成，扩大修改空间并实现更完整的语义对齐，在多个基准测试上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型（UMMs）在文本到图像生成中采用精炼-通过-编辑（RvE）范式，存在两个关键局限：1）编辑指令只能粗略描述提示-图像之间的不对齐，导致精炼不完整；2）像素级的内容保留虽对编辑必要，但不必要地限制了有效修改空间。因此需要一种能克服这些限制的新精炼框架。

Method: 提出Refinement via Regeneration (RvR)框架，将精炼重新定义为条件图像再生而非编辑。RvR不依赖编辑指令和严格的内容保留约束，而是以目标提示和初始图像的语义token为条件来再生图像，从而在更大的修改空间中实现更完整的语义对齐。

Result: Geneval从0.78提升至0.91，DPGBench从84.02提升至87.21，UniGenBench++从61.53提升至77.41，充分验证了RvR的有效性。

Conclusion: RvR通过将精炼从编辑范式重构为再生范式，有效克服了RvE中编辑指令粗糙和像素级保留限制修改空间的问题，显著提升了统一多模态模型在文本到图像生成中的性能上限。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) integrate visual understanding and generation within a single framework. For text-to-image (T2I) tasks, this unified capability allows UMMs to refine outputs after their initial generation, potentially extending the performance upper bound. Current UMM-based refinement methods primarily follow a refinement-via-editing (RvE) paradigm, where UMMs produce editing instructions to modify misaligned regions while preserving aligned content. However, editing instructions often describe prompt-image misalignment only coarsely, leading to incomplete refinement. Moreover, pixel-level preservation, though necessary for editing, unnecessarily restricts the effective modification space for refinement. To address these limitations, we propose Refinement via Regeneration (RvR), a novel framework that reformulates refinement as conditional image regeneration rather than editing. Instead of relying on editing instructions and enforcing strict content preservation, RvR regenerates images conditioned on the target prompt and the semantic tokens of the initial image, enabling more complete semantic alignment with a larger modification space. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of RvR, improving Geneval from 0.78 to 0.91, DPGBench from 84.02 to 87.21, and UniGenBench++ from 61.53 to 77.41.

</details>


### [86] [SAMe: A Semantic Anatomy Mapping Engine for Robotic Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25646)
*Jing Zhang,Duojie Chen,Wentao Jiang,Zihan Lou,Jianxin Liu,Xinwu Cui,Qinghong Zhao,Bo Du,Christoph F. Dietrich,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAMe（语义解剖映射引擎），为机器人超声提供显式解剖先验层，通过“目标-解剖-动作”流程解决扫描初始化问题：将非特异性临床主诉转化为目标器官，从单张外部身体图像实例化患者特定解剖表示，并翻译为6自由度探头初始化状态，无需术前CT/MRI配准。在真实机器人实验中，肝脏初始化器官命中率达97.3%，肾脏为81.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人超声系统缺乏解剖理解能力，无法自主确定扫描目标、起始位置及适应患者个体解剖差异，仍然依赖专家干预来启动扫描。

Method: SAMe引擎包含三个步骤：(1) 语义接地：将模糊的临床主诉映射到结构化目标器官；(2) 解剖实例化：从单张外部身体图像为接地目标构建患者特定解剖表示；(3) 动作翻译：将解剖表示转换为控制层面的6自由度探头初始化状态，无需术前CT/MRI配准。

Result: 真实机器人实验中，肝脏初始化器官命中率97.3%，肾脏81.7%；在限制为质心目标时，SAMe仍优于表面启发式基线。单器官推理仅需0.08秒。

Conclusion: SAMe建立了一个显式解剖先验层，有效解决了扫描初始化问题，为基于临床主诉、解剖信息驱动的自主机器人超声扫描提供了解剖学基础，可支撑更广泛的下游自主扫描流程。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound has advanced local image-driven control, contact regulation, and view optimization, yet current systems lack the anatomical understanding needed to determine what to scan, where to begin, and how to adapt to individual patient anatomy. These gaps make systems still reliant on expert intervention to initiate scanning. Here we present SAMe, a semantic anatomy mapping engine that provides robotic ultrasound with an explicit anatomical prior layer. SAMe addresses scan initiation as a target-to-anatomy-to-action process: it grounds under-specified clinical complaints into structured target organs, instantiates a patient-specific anatomical representation for the grounded targets from a single external body image, and translates this representation into control-facing 6-DoF probe initialization states without any additional registration using preoperative CT or MRI. The anatomical representation maintained by SAMe is explicit, lightweight (single-organ inference in 0.08s), and compatible with downstream control by design. Across semantic grounding, anatomical instantiation, and real-robot evaluation, SAMe shows strong performance across the full initialization pipeline. In real-robot experiments, SAMe achieved overall organ-hit rates of 97.3% for liver initialization and 81.7% for kidney initialization across the evaluated target sets. Even when restricted to the centroid target, SAMe outperformed the surface-heuristic baseline for both liver and kidney initialization. These results establish an explicit anatomical prior layer that addresses scan initialization and is designed to support broader downstream autonomous scanning pipelines, providing the anatomical foundation for complaint-driven, anatomically informed robotic ultrasonography.

</details>


### [87] [Exploring Remote Photoplethysmography for Neonatal Pain Detection from Facial Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25680)
*Ashutosh Dhamaniya,Anup Kumar Gupta,Trishna Saikia,Puneet Gupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于远程光电容积描记法(rPPG)的非接触式新生儿疼痛检测方法，通过质量参数筛选受皮肤变形影响最小的ROI区域，并采用信噪比作为适应度参数提取高质量rPPG信号，实验表明蓝色通道信号效果最佳，且rPPG与音频特征融合效果优于单一模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿未治疗的疼痛会导致发育迟缓和体重增长减缓等不良后果，而传统接触式生理参数估计方法不适合长期监测且存在疾病传播风险（如COVID-19），因此需要更客观、可靠的非接触式疼痛评估方法。

Method: 采用远程光电容积描记法(rPPG)非接触式估计脉搏信号；引入质量参数选择受皮肤变形影响最小的ROI区域；使用信噪比作为适应度参数提取噪声影响最小的rPPG信号；将rPPG与音频特征进行多模态融合。

Result: rPPG信号能提供有用的新生儿疼痛检测信息；蓝色通道提取的信号优于其他颜色通道；rPPG与音频特征融合比单一模态效果更好。

Conclusion: 非接触式rPPG方法可用于新生儿疼痛检测，多模态融合（rPPG+音频）能提升检测性能，为NICU提供更安全可靠的自动化疼痛评估方案。

Abstract: Unaddressed pain in neonates can lead to adverse effects, including delayed development and slower weight gain, emphasising the need for more objective and reliable pain assessment methods. Hence, automated methods using behavioural and physiological pain indicators have been developed to aid healthcare professionals in the Neonatal ICU. Traditional contact-based methods for physiological parameter estimation are unsuitable for long-term monitoring and increase the risk of spreading diseases like COVID-19. We introduce a novel approach using remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) to estimate pulse signals in a non-contact manner and employ them for neonatal pain detection. The temporal signals acquired from regions-of-interest (ROIs) affected by skin deformations may exhibit lower quality and provide erroneous rPPG signals. Therefore, we incorporated a quality parameter to select the temporal signals obtained from ROIs that are least affected by skin deformations. Further, we employed signal-to-noise ratio as a fitness parameter to extract the rPPG signal corresponding to the clip that is least affected by noise. Experimental findings demonstrate that the rPPG signals provide useful information for neonatal pain detection, and signals extracted from the blue colour channel outperform those extracted from other colour channels. We also show that combining rPPG and audio features provides better results than individual modalities.

</details>


### [88] [QB-LIF: Learnable-Scale Quantized Burst Neurons for Efficient SNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25688)
*Dewei Bai,Hongxiang Peng,Jiajun Mei,Yang Ren,Hong Qu,Dawen Xia,Zhang Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出QB-LIF神经元，将脉冲爆发重新定义为带可学习尺度的饱和均匀量化，解决了二进制脉冲编码的信息吞吐量瓶颈。通过可吸收尺度策略和ReLSG-ET替代梯度，在保持纯累加执行范式的同时，实现了超低延迟下比二进制和固定爆发SNN更高的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制脉冲编码的1-bit每时间步表示从根本上限制了信息吞吐量，这一瓶颈在深度架构和短仿真时间步下尤为突出，亟需一种既能提升信息容量又能保持神经形态硬件兼容性的脉冲编码机制。

Method: 1) QB-LIF神经元：将爆发脉冲建模为膜电位的饱和均匀量化，量化尺度作为可学习参数，每层自适应调整脉冲分辨率；2) 可吸收尺度策略：推理时将学习到的量化尺度折叠到突触权重中，维持纯累加执行范式；3) ReLSG-ET替代梯度：带指数尾部的整流线性替代梯度，在爆发间隔内维持梯度流动，实现离散多级空间的稳定优化。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100、ImageNet等静态基准和CIFAR10-DVS、DVS128-Gesture等事件驱动基准上，QB-LIF在超低延迟条件下一致优于二进制和固定爆发SNN，精度显著提升。

Conclusion: QB-LIF通过在爆发脉冲中引入可学习量化尺度，有效克服了二进制SNN的信息瓶颈，同时通过可吸收策略和定制化替代梯度保持了神经形态计算的高效性和硬件友好性，为低延迟高精度SNN提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: Binary spike coding enables sparse and event-driven computation in spiking neural networks (SNNs), yet its 1-bit-per-timestep representation fundamentally limits information throughput. This bottleneck becomes increasingly restrictive in deep architectures under short simulation horizons. We propose the Quantized Burst-LIF (QB-LIF) neuron, which reformulates burst spiking as a saturated uniform quantization of membrane potentials with a learnable scale. Instead of relying on predefined multi-threshold structures, QB-LIF treats the quantization scale as a trainable parameter, allowing each layer to autonomously adapt its spiking resolution to the underlying membrane-potential statistics. To preserve hardware efficiency, we introduce an absorbable scale strategy that folds the learned quantized scale into synaptic weights during inference, maintaining a strict accumulate-only (AC) execution paradigm. To enable stable optimization in the discrete multi-level space, we further design ReLSG-ET, a rectified-linear surrogate gradient with exponential tails that sustains gradient flow across burst intervals. Extensive experiments on static (CIFAR-10/100, ImageNet) and event-driven (CIFAR10-DVS, DVS128-Gesture) benchmarks demonstrate that QB-LIF consistently outperforms binary and fixed-burst SNNs, achieving higher accuracy under ultra-low latency while preserving neuromorphic compatibility.

</details>


### [89] [Toward Multimodal Conversational AI for Age-Related Macular Degeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25720)
*Ran Gu,Benjamin Hou,Mélanie Hébert,Asmita Indurkar,Yifan Yang,Emily Y. Chew,Tiarnán D. L. Keenan,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OcularChat，一个基于Qwen2.5-VL微调的多模态大语言模型，利用70余万模拟医患对话和46,167张眼底彩照进行训练，在AMD诊断分类任务中显著优于现有MLLM，并在客观分类性能、诊断推理能力和临床交互对话方面均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在视网膜疾病检测中表现强劲，但多数系统仅提供静态预测，缺乏临床推理和交互式解释能力，无法有效支持临床决策和患者咨询。

Method: 基于Qwen2.5-VL模型，使用705,850条模拟医患对话和46,167张眼底彩照进行微调，通过视觉问答方式训练模型识别AMD关键特征并生成带推理过程的诊断预测。

Result: 在AREDS数据集中，OcularChat对晚期AMD、色素异常和玻璃膜疣大小三个诊断任务的准确率分别达到0.954、0.849和0.678，显著优于现有MLLM；在AREDS2上保持最佳性能；三位独立眼科医生评估中，OcularChat在所有临床评分维度上均优于强基线模型（5分量表上晚期AMD 3.503 vs. 2.833，色素异常3.272 vs. 2.828，玻璃膜疣大小3.064 vs. 2.433，总体印象2.978 vs. 2.464）。

Conclusion: 多模态大语言模型能够实现准确、可解释且临床实用的眼底图像AMD诊断与分类，具备诊断推理、临床相关解释和交互式对话能力。

Abstract: Despite strong performance of deep learning models in retinal disease detection, most systems produce static predictions without clinical reasoning or interactive explanation. Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) integrate diagnostic predictions with clinically meaningful dialogue to support clinical decision-making and patient counseling. In this study, OcularChat, an MLLM, was fine-tuned from Qwen2.5-VL using simulated patient-physician dialogues to diagnose age-related macular degeneration (AMD) through visual question answering on color fundus photographs (CFPs). A total of 705,850 simulated dialogues paired with 46,167 CFPs were generated to train OcularChat to identify key AMD features and produce reasoned predictions. OcularChat demonstrated strong classification performance in AREDS, achieving accuracies of 0.954, 0.849, and 0.678 for the three diagnostic tasks: advanced AMD, pigmentary abnormalities, and drusen size, significantly outperforming existing MLLMs. On AREDS2, OcularChat remained the top-performing method on all tasks. Across three independent ophthalmologist graders, OcularChat achieved higher mean scores than a strong baseline model for advanced AMD (3.503 vs. 2.833), pigmentary abnormalities (3.272 vs. 2.828), drusen size (3.064 vs. 2.433), and overall impression (2.978 vs. 2.464) on a 5-point clinical grading rubric. Beyond strong objective performance in AMD severity classification, OcularChat demonstrated the ability to provide diagnostic reasoning, clinically relevant explanations, and interactive dialogue, with high performance in subjective ophthalmologist evaluation. These findings suggest that MLLMs may enable accurate, interpretable, and clinically useful image-based diagnosis and classification of AMD.

</details>


### [90] [Sketch2Arti: Sketch-based Articulation Modeling of CAD Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25781)
*Yi Yang,Hao Pan,Yijing Cui,Alla Sheffer,Changjian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sketch2Arti是一个基于草图的三维CAD物体关节建模系统，用户通过2D草图指定运动意图，系统自动识别可动部件并预测运动参数，支持多关节迭代建模和壳模型内部补全，训练无需类别信息，具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计师通过轻量级草图（如箭头和笔触）自然表达关节运动意图，但将此类草图转化为关节化三维模型仍主要依赖手动操作，缺乏自动化的草图驱动关节建模方法。

Method: 给定CAD模型和用户从选定视角绘制的2D草图，Sketch2Arti自动发现对应的可动部件并预测其运动参数；采用无类别信息的训练方式，支持跨类别泛化；对缺乏内部结构的壳模型，通过用户草图引导实现可控的内部补全。

Result: 综合实验和用户评估证明了Sketch2Arti在有效性、可控性和泛化性方面的优势。

Conclusion: Sketch2Arti首次实现了基于草图的CAD物体关节建模，弥合了2D草图与3D关节模型之间的鸿沟，为交互式动画、仿真和形状编辑提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Articulation modeling aims to infer movable parts and their motion parameters for a 3D object, enabling interactive animation, simulation, and shape editing. In this paper, we present Sketch2Arti, the first sketch-based articulation modeling system for CAD objects. Our key observation is that designers naturally communicate articulation intent through lightweight sketches (e.g., arrows and strokes) that indicate how parts should move, yet translating such sketches into articulated 3D models remains largely manual. Sketch2Arti bridges this gap by enabling users to specify articulation through simple 2D sketches drawn from a chosen viewpoint. Given a CAD model and user sketches, our approach automatically discovers the corresponding movable parts and predicts their motion parameters, allowing iterative modeling of multiple articulations on complex objects with fine-grained control. Importantly, Sketch2Arti is trained in a category-agnostic manner without requiring object category information, leading to strong generalization to diverse objects beyond existing articulation datasets. Moreover, for shell models lacking interior structures, Sketch2Arti supports controllable internal completion guided by user sketches, generating plausible internal components consistent with the existing geometry and predicted motion constraints. Comprehensive experiments and user evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness, controllability, and generalization of Sketch2Arti. The code, dataset, and the prototype system are at https://arlo-yang.github.io/Sketch2Arti.

</details>


### [91] [Instruction-Evidence Contrastive Dual-Stream Decoding for Grounded Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25809)
*Yashwant Pravinrao Bangde,Debaditya Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IECD2解码框架，通过指令驱动流和证据驱动流的对比解码，平衡语言信息性与视觉忠实性，有效减少多模态大模型的幻觉问题，在多个视觉-语言推理任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型虽能生成流畅输出，但常缺乏视觉证据支撑；指令提示进一步放大了语言先验偏差，尤其在视觉信号不确定时加剧了幻觉问题。

Method: 提出指令-证据对比双流解码（IECD2），维护两个并行概率分布：指令驱动流促进信息丰富响应，证据驱动流强制视觉对齐；通过对称KL散度对比门自适应融合两流，抑制语言先验占优但视觉证据不足的token。

Result: 在POPE、MME、VQAv2、AMBER、MS-COCO、LLaVA-Bench等数据集上，IECD2在字幕生成、视觉问答等任务中显著提升了准确率和推理性能，并在所有评估指标上大幅降低了幻觉。

Conclusion: 对比双流解码有效平衡了语言先验与视觉证据，为缓解多模态大模型幻觉提供了可行的解码级解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit strong performance in instruction following and open-ended vision-language reasoning, yet they frequently generate fluent outputs that are weakly grounded in visual evidence. Prior works have shown that instruction prompting further worsens this issue by amplifying language priors, especially when the visual signal is uncertain or ambiguous. To address this challenge, we propose a decoding framework that explicitly balances linguistic informativeness and visual faithfulness during generation. Our method, Instruction-Evidence Contrastive Dual-Stream Decoding (IECD2), maintains two parallel probability distributions of tokens at each decoding step: an instruction-driven stream that promotes expressive and informative responses, and an evidence-driven stream that enforces strict grounding in the image. These two streams are adaptively fused using a symmetric KL-based contrast-based gate, which suppresses tokens favored by language priors but unsupported by visual evidence, while preserving them when both distributions agree. We evaluate IECD2 on multiple datasets spanning various generative vision-language reasoning tasks such as captioning and visual question answering, including POPE, MME, VQAv2, AMBER, MS-COCO, and LLaVA-Bench. IECD2 demonstrates consistent improvements in task accuracy and reasoning performance, alongside a substantial reduction in hallucination across all evaluation metrics compared to state-of-the-art decoding approaches.

</details>


### [92] [Mutual Forcing: Dual-Mode Self-Evolution for Fast Autoregressive Audio-Video Character Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25819)
*Yupeng Zhou,Lianghua Huang,Zhifan Wu,Jiabao Wang,Yupeng Shi,Biao Jiang,Daquan Zhou,Yu Liu,Ming-Ming Cheng,Qibin Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Mutual Forcing框架，通过两阶段训练（先单模态后联合）和单模型共享参数的少步/多步自蒸馏机制，实现高效的自回归音频-视频生成，在4-8步内达到或超越基线50步的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频-视频生成面临两大挑战：联合建模困难（优化难度大）和流式生成效率低（传统方法需先训练双向模型再多阶段蒸馏为因果模型，流程复杂且开销大）。

Method: 1) 两阶段训练：先独立训练音频和视频单模态生成器，再耦合为统一模型在配对数据上联合训练；2) Mutual Forcing：在原生自回归模型中同时集成少步（few-step）和多步（multi-step）生成，二者共享参数。多步模式通过自蒸馏提升少步模式，少步模式在训练时生成历史上下文改善训练-推理一致性，两者相互强化。

Result: Mutual Forcing在仅用4-8个采样步的情况下，匹配或超越了需要约50步的强基线方法，在效率和生成质量上均表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: Mutual Forcing无需额外双向教师模型，支持更灵活的训练序列长度，降低训练开销，且能从真实配对数据中直接学习改进，为高效流式音频-视频生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: In this work, we propose Mutual Forcing, a framework for fast autoregressive audio-video generation with long-horizon audio-video synchronization. Our approach addresses two key challenges: joint audio-video modeling and fast autoregressive generation. To ease joint audio-video optimization, we adopt a two-stage training strategy: we first train uni-modal generators and then couple them into a unified audio-video model for joint training on paired data. For streaming generation, we ask whether a native fast causal audio-video model can be trained directly, instead of following existing streaming distillation pipelines that typically train a bidirectional model first and then convert it into a causal generator through multiple distillation stages. Our answer is Mutual Forcing, which builds directly on native autoregressive model and integrates few-step and multi-step generation within a single weight-shared model, enabling self-distillation and improved training-inference consistency. The multi-step mode improves the few-step mode via self-distillation, while the few-step mode generates historical context during training to improve training-inference consistency; because the two modes share parameters, these two effects reinforce each other within a single model. Compared with prior approaches such as Self-Forcing, Mutual Forcing removes the need for an additional bidirectional teacher model, supports more flexible training sequence lengths, reduces training overhead, and allows the model to improve directly from real paired data rather than a fixed teacher. Experiments show that Mutual Forcing matches or surpasses strong baselines that require around 50 sampling steps while using only 4 to 8 steps, demonstrating substantial advantages in both efficiency and quality. The project page is available at https://mutualforcing.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Semantic Denial of Service in LLM-controlled robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24790)
*Jonathan Steinberg,Oren Gal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现，用于确保机器人安全的指令跟随机制存在语义拒绝服务攻击漏洞：攻击者通过向音频通道注入1-5个短的安全类短语（如“停止”），即可触发模型的安全推理，导致机器人中断或停止执行，而无需破解模型或覆盖其策略。即使采用提示级防御，攻击形式仍会转换为确认循环或虚假警报，防御只是改变了中断形式而非消除中断。实验表明，多样的攻击注入比重复同一短语更有效，根本解决需要架构级改进而非提示级修补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全导向的指令跟随本应用于确保机器人安全，但作者发现这种机制存在可利用的攻击面。攻击者可以利用模型的"安全推理"能力，通过注入看似合法的安全提示来破坏机器人任务执行，即使不进行传统越狱也能造成拒绝服务。

Method: 在四种视觉语言模型、七种提示级防御策略、三种部署模式以及单次/多次注入设置下进行系统实验。使用Disruption Success Rate (DSR)衡量攻击效果，比较不同防御策略对攻击抑制与真实危险响应之间的权衡。

Result: 提示级防御可以降低某些模型上的硬停止攻击成功率，但无法消除中断本身，而是将其转化为确认循环和虚假警报。多样化的注入短语比重复同一短语更有效，因为模型将多样化的安全提示视为相互佐证的证据。

Conclusion: 系统架构层面需要改进：将未认证的音频文本直接输入LLM会在安全监控与动作选择之间产生可避免的安全依赖关系。单纯的提示级防御不足以解决这一根本问题。

Abstract: Safety-oriented instruction-following is supposed to keep LLM-controlled robots safe. We show it also creates an availability attack surface. By injecting short safety-plausible phrases (1-5 tokens) into a robots audio channel, an adversary can trigger the models safety reasoning to halt or disrupt execution without jailbreaking the model or overriding its policy. In the embodied setting, this is a semantic denial-of-service attack: the agent stops because the injected signal looks like a legitimate alert. Across four vision-language models, seven prompt-level defenses, three deployment modes, and single- and multi-injection settings, we find that prompt-only defenses trade off attack suppression against genuine hazard response. The strongest defenses reduce hard-stop attack success on some models, but defenses change the form of disruption, not its fact: suppressed hard stops re-emerge as acknowledge loops and false alerts, which we measure with Disruption Success Rate (DSR). We further find that injection variety is consistently more effective than repeating the same phrase, suggesting that models treat diverse safety cues as corroborating evidence. The practical implication is architectural rather than prompt-level: systems that route unauthenticated audio text directly into the LLM create an avoidable security dependency between safety monitoring and action selection.

</details>


### [94] [A Comparative Evaluation of AI Agent Security Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24826)
*Qi Li,Jiu Li,Pingtao Wei,Jianjun Xu,Xueyi Wei,Jiwei Shi,Xuan Zhang,Yanhui Yang,Xiaodong Hui,Peng Xu,Lingquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对 DKnownAI Guard 与 AWS Bedrock Guardrails、Azure Content Safety、Lakera Guard 三款竞品在 AI Agent 安全场景下进行对比评估，以人工标注为基准，检测两类风险：针对 Agent 自身的威胁（指令覆盖、间接注入、工具滥用）和有害内容请求（仇恨言论、色情、暴力）。结果显示 DKnownAI Guard 召回率最高（96.5%），真负率第一（90.4%），综合表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI Agent 面临多种安全威胁（指令覆盖、间接注入、工具滥用等），亟需有效的防护措施。本文旨在通过系统化的对比评估，衡量不同安全防护产品在检测 Agent 自身威胁和有害内容方面的实际性能。

Method: 采用人工标注作为 ground truth，对四款安全防护产品（DKnownAI Guard、AWS Bedrock Guardrails、Azure Content Safety、Lakera Guard）进行对比评估，分别测试其对 Agent 自身威胁和有害内容请求的检测能力。

Result: DKnownAI Guard 召回率 96.5%（最高），真负率（TNR）90.4%（第一），综合性能在所有评估的防护产品中表现最佳。

Conclusion: DKnownAI Guard 在 AI Agent 安全防护的对比评估中综合表现最优，能够有效检测 Agent 自身威胁和有害内容请求。

Abstract: This report presents a comparative evaluation of DKnownAI Guard in AI agent security scenarios, benchmarked against three competing products: AWS Bedrock Guardrails, Azure Content Safety, and Lakera Guard. Using human annotation as the ground truth, we assess each guardrail's ability to detect two categories of risks: threats to the agent itself (e.g., instruction override, indirect injection, tool abuse) and requests intended to elicit harmful content (e.g., hate speech, pornography, violence). Evaluation results demonstrate that DKnownAI Guard achieves the highest recall rate at 96.5\% and ranks first in true negative rate (TNR) at 90.4\%, delivering the best overall performance among all evaluated guardrails.

</details>


### [95] [Network Impact of Post-Quantum Certificate Chain sizes on Time to First Byte in TLS Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24869)
*Matthew Chou,Phuong Cao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了后量子密码（PQC）迁移对CDN环境下TLS握手延迟（TTFB）的影响，发现证书链大小超过传输层飞行限制时TTFB会显著增加。通过对比ECDSA和ML-DSA证书方案，以及CDN优化（会话恢复、证书大小优化、地理分布）与Merkle Tree证书（MTC），发现MTC可使可支持的证书链大小提升2-3倍，而CDN优化仅支持约1.6倍的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算机的发展，传统X.509密码算法面临安全威胁，PQC迁移成为必然。但PQC证书链大小相比经典算法增长5倍至20倍以上，可能严重影响互联网基础设施性能。本文旨在量化PQC迁移对CDN场景下TLS握手延迟的影响，并探索缓解策略。

Method: 在CDN聚焦的TLS条件下评估TTFB；对比ECDSA和ML-DSA证书方案，通过受控添加证书扩展生成相似大小的证书链；利用Zeek监控NCSA高性能计算系统的TLS流量以测量真实会话恢复率；比较CDN优化与MTC在减少证书链大小方面的效果。

Result: 证书链大小超过传输层飞行限制时TTFB出现离散性增加；MTC可支持2-3倍的证书链大小增长；CDN优化支持约1.6倍的证书链大小增长，效果有限。

Conclusion: MTC在保持证书链低于飞行限制阈值方面显著优于CDN优化，是应对PQC证书链膨胀问题更有效的方案。

Abstract: Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) is a rapidly growing deployment challenge as cryptographically relevant quantum computers (CRQC) continue to advance, leaving traditional cryptographic algorithms used in X.509 vulnerable to attack. However, PQC introduces significant deployment challenges in real-world networks, with handshake sizes increasing from 5x to over 20x compared to classical algorithms. In this work, we evaluate the time to first byte (TTFB) under CDN-focused TLS conditions to characterize the latency cost of transitioning existing internet infrastructure to quantum-safe certificate schemes. We observe discrete increases in TTFB as certificate chain sizes exceed transport layer data flight limits. To isolate the impact of certificate chains, we evaluate both ECDSA and ML-DSA-based certificate schemes, generating similarly sized certificate chains through controlled addition of certificate extensions. We additionally examine how CDN properties such as session resumption, certificate size optimizations, and geographical distribution reduce latency penalties. We utilize Zeek-monitored TLS traffic through a High-Performance Computing System (NCSA) with terabyte network connectivity across the nation to quantify real-world session resumption rates. We compare CDN-driven size optimization with Merkle Tree Certificates (MTC) to examine how size reductions allow certificate chains to remain under the flight limit threshold. We find that MTC allows for 2x-3x increase in supportable certificate chain size, whereas CDN-based optimizations yield more limited reductions, supporting up to approximately 1.6x certificate chain size increase.

</details>


### [96] [Verifying Provenance of Digital Media: Why the C2PA Specifications Fall Short](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24890)
*Enis Golaszewski,Neal Krawetz,Alan T. Sherman,Edward Zieglar,Sai K. Matukumalli,Roberto Yus,Carson L. Kegley,Michael Barthel,William Bowman,Bharg Barot,Kaur Kullman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对C2PA（内容来源与真实性联盟）系统进行了全面的独立安全分析，发现其核心协议存在安全缺陷，无法达到声称的安全目标，在金融披露、新闻或法律证据等高风险场景中尚不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速发展使得大规模创建令人信服的伪造媒体变得容易，为此工业联盟开发了C2PA系统以提供数字内容的可验证来源。研究者希望独立评估该系统是否真正实现了其安全承诺。

Method: 研究团队对C2PA进行了首次全面的独立安全分析，包括对C2PA核心协议的形式化方法分析。

Result: 当前C2PA规范未能实现其声称的安全目标，也未能实现所有此类来源系统可靠部署所需的关键附加目标。

Conclusion: C2PA是一个有前景的想法，但在高风险场景（如金融披露、新闻或法律证据）中尚不应被依赖，否则可能误导用户、平台和政策制定者。

Abstract: The rapid rise of generative AI has made it easy to create convincing fake media at scale. In response, an industrial coalition has developed the Coalition for Content Provenance and Authenticity (C2PA), a system intended to provide verifiable provenance for digital content. Our research team conducted the first comprehensive, independent security analysis of C2PA. Our study includes the first formal-methods analysis of C2PA's core protocols. We find that the current C2PA specifications fail to achieve their claimed security goals. Furthermore, they also fail to achieve key additional goals, which all such provenance systems require for trustworthy deployment. As a result, C2PA may mislead users, platforms, and policymakers if relied upon prematurely. C2PA is a promising idea, but it should not yet be relied upon for high-stakes uses such as financial disclosures, journalism, or legal evidence.

</details>


### [97] [CAN-QA: A Question-Answering Benchmark for Reasoning over In-Vehicle CAN Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24935)
*Jing Chen,Abhijay Deevi,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CAN-QA基准，将CAN总线入侵检测重新定义为问答任务，包含33,128个QA对，实验发现大语言模型在时间推理和多条件推断方面存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAN入侵检测方法将问题建模为分类任务，忽略了CAN流量的时间和关系结构，且与现实取证工作流不匹配，缺乏系统性的流量行为推理能力。

Method: 将原始CAN日志分割为时间窗口，采用确定性规则模板生成自然语言问题及自动标注的答案，构建了包含33,128个QA对、10个类别的CAN-QA数据集，并评估了大语言模型在True/False和选择题上的表现。

Result: 大语言模型能捕捉表面统计规律，但在时间推理、多条件推断和高层次行为解释方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: CAN-QA为CAN流量分析提供了新的问答范式基准，揭示了大语言模型在该任务中的局限性，代码已开源。

Abstract: The Controller Area Network (CAN) is a safety-critical in-vehicle communication protocol that lacks built-in security mechanisms, making intrusion detection essential. Existing approaches predominantly formulate CAN intrusion detection as a classification task, mapping complex traffic patterns to attack labels. However, this formulation abstracts away the temporal and relational structure of CAN traffic and misaligns with real-world forensic workflows, which require systematic reasoning about traffic behavior. To address this gap, we introduce CAN-QA, the first benchmark that reformulates CAN traffic analysis as a question-answering (QA) task. CAN-QA converts raw CAN logs into temporally segmented windows and applies deterministic rule-based templates to generate natural-language questions paired with automatically derived ground-truth answers. The resulting dataset comprises 33,128 QA pairs across 10 categories, each targeting distinct semantic and temporal properties of CAN traffic. Using CAN-QA, we evaluate large language models across both True/False and multiple-choice formats. Our results indicate that, although these models capture superficial statistical regularities, they struggle with temporal reasoning, multi-condition inference, and higher-level behavioral interpretation. Our code is available at https://github.com/Kriiiiss/CAN-QA.

</details>


### [98] [Poisoning Learned Index Structures: Static and Dynamic Adversarial Attacks on ALEX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24975)
*Allen Jue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了ALEX动态学习索引在静态数据投毒和动态算法复杂度攻击两种威胁模型下的鲁棒性，发现动态ACA攻击可导致2-2.8倍查询吞吐量下降，但攻击效果高度依赖数据分布，密集键分布会限制攻击有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习索引依赖数据分布（CDF建模）导致其对对抗性操纵存在潜在脆弱性，而现有研究对静态投毒和动态算法复杂度攻击的评估规模有限且多仅考虑单一威胁模型，缺乏统一、可复现的对比框架。

Method: 在统一可复现框架下，对ALEX学习索引进行两类攻击实验：静态数据投毒与动态算法复杂度攻击（ACA），使用SOSD多数据集（含真实键基线），扩展至20万条对抗性插入，通过控制工作负载隔离攻击效应。

Result: 静态投毒在批量加载场景下对ALEX查询性能影响极小；动态ACA可导致高达2-2.8倍的查询吞吐量下降；攻击效果高度依赖数据集，密集键分布由于重复插入多和ALEX局部化结构限制了攻击有效性。

Conclusion: 学习索引的鲁棒性关键取决于威胁模型、数据分布和评估方法三者的交互作用；研究强调了对控制工作负载的需求，以及局部结构损伤与全局查询指标之间的不匹配问题。

Abstract: Learned index structures achieve high performance by modeling the cumulative distribution function (CDF) of keys, but this reliance on data distributions introduces potential vulnerability to adversarial manipulation. Prior work has explored both static data poisoning and dynamic algorithmic complexity attacks (ACA), though evaluations are typically limited in scale or consider only one threat model. We present a systematic study of both attack paradigms on ALEX, a state-of-the-art dynamic learned index, under a unified and reproducible framework.
  Our evaluation scales to realistic workloads with up to 200K adversarial inserts and includes multiple SOSD datasets with diverse key distributions, as well as a real-key baseline to isolate adversarial effects. Our results show a clear separation between threat models. Static poisoning has minimal impact on lookup performance in ALEX under bulk-loaded settings, while dynamic ACA induces substantial degradation, with up to 2--2.8x slowdown in lookup throughput. However, attack effectiveness is highly dataset-dependent: dense key distributions limit adversarial leverage due to duplicate-heavy insertions and ALEX's localized structure.
  We highlight key evaluation considerations, including the need for control workloads and the mismatch between localized structural damage and global query metrics. These results show that robustness in learned indexes depends critically on the interaction between threat model, data distribution, and evaluation methodology.

</details>


### [99] [Extended Abstract: Shaperd: Easily Adoptable Real-Time Traffic Shaper for Fully Encrypted Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25069)
*Sarah Wilson,Stella Tian,Sina Kamali*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Shaperd——一种实时流量塑形工具，帮助全加密协议规避审查，通过约束系统实时修改数据包内容，产生任意特征的流量，且开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全加密协议（FEPs）用于规避审查，但近年来审查者通过被动流量分析和主动探测攻击成功封锁了这些协议，亟需一种易于部署且实时的防御手段。

Method: Shaperd直接在数据包内容上进行实时操作，采用新颖的约束系统，允许用户生成具有任意期望特征的流量流。

Result: 初步结果显示Shaperd对底层系统吞吐量引入的开销极小。

Conclusion: Shaperd是一种易采用、实时的流量塑形工具，能有效帮助全加密协议提升抗检测能力。

Abstract: Fully encrypted protocol-based tools (FEPs) are tools commonly used to circumvent censorship in restrictive regions, valued for their performance and security. However, in recent years, censors have been able to block them using an array of attacks based on passive traffic analysis and active probing. We propose Shaperd, an easily adoptable and real-time traffic shaper designed specifically to aid FEPs become more resilient to detection. Shaperd operates directly on packet contents in real time, using a novel constraint system to allow its users to generate traffic flows with any desired features. Our preliminary results reveal Shaperd introduces minimal overhead to the underlying system's throughput.

</details>


### [100] [Scalable Secure Biometric Authentication without Auxiliary Identifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25071)
*Alexander Bienstock,Daniel Escudero,Antigoni Polychroniadou,Zhen Zeng,Pranav Bhat,Ashok Singal,Prashant Sharma,Manuela Veloso*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型生物特征认证系统，通过融合人工智能与高级密码学技术，在保护云数据库中用户生物特征数据安全的同时，实现了可扩展性和高性能，首次证明了无需辅助标识符的可扩展隐私保护生物特征认证的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物特征认证系统在云数据库场景下存在严重安全漏洞，数据泄露会导致所有用户敏感生物特征数据被破解；而现有解决方案要么安全性不足，要么因计算开销过高而无法实际部署使用。

Method: 将人工智能与高级密码学技术以新颖方式相结合，并在此过程中提供了多项优化。

Result: 首次证明了无需辅助标识符的真实世界可扩展隐私保护生物特征认证是可行的。

Conclusion: 该系统具备可证明的数据泄露安全保障，同时保持了可扩展性和高性能，有望推动工业界广泛采用及相关领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: The prevalence of biometric authentication has been on the rise due to its ease of use and elimination of weak passwords. To date, most biometric authentication systems have been designed for on-device authentication of the device owner (e.g., smartphones and laptops). Recently, biometric authentication systems have started to emerge that are designed to authenticate users against cloud databases storing representations of biometrics for large numbers of users (potentially millions), such as those facilitating biometric payments. However, the use of a large cloud database introduces a significant attack vector, as a breach of the database could lead to the compromise of all enrolled users' sensitive biometric data. Indeed, all such existing systems either do not adequately protect against such a breach, or are impractical to deploy and use due to their high computational overhead. In this work, we present a new biometric authentication system that provides provable security guarantees against data breaches, while remaining scalable and performant. To do so, we marry artificial intelligence with advanced cryptographic techniques in a novel fashion, providing several optimizations along the way. Our work is the first to show that real-world scalable privacy-preserving biometric authentication without auxiliary identifiers is feasible, and we believe that it will spur widespread industrial adoption and further research in this area.

</details>


### [101] [Structured Security Auditing and Robustness Enhancement for Untrusted Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25109)
*Lijia Lv,Xuehai Tang,Jie Wen,Jizhong Han,Songlin Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillGuard-Robust，一种针对不可信Agent Skills的预加载审计框架，通过角色感知证据提取、选择性语义验证和一致性保持裁决，将审计转化为鲁棒的三分类任务。在404个包的聚合测试中，整体精确匹配率达97.30%，恶意风险召回率98.33%，攻击精确一致性98.89%；在254个包的外部生态测试中分别达到99.66%、100.00%和100.00%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防护措施在语义等价改写下对恶意意图的恢复不一致，且预加载审计从单提示过滤转变为跨文件安全检查，需要更鲁棒的审计方法。

Method: 将预加载审计形式化为鲁棒的三分类任务，提出SkillGuard-Robust框架，包含角色感知证据提取、选择性语义验证和一致性保持裁决三个组件。

Result: 在404个包的聚合视图上，整体精确匹配率97.30%，恶意风险召回率98.33%，攻击精确一致性98.89%。在254个包的外部生态视图上，分别达到99.66%、100.00%和100.00%。

Conclusion: 分解式包审计能够显著提升冻结模型和公共生态的鲁棒性，但更严苛的外部源迁移仍是一个开放挑战。

Abstract: Agent Skills package SKILL.md files, scripts, reference documents, and repository context into reusable capability units, turning pre-load auditing from single-prompt filtering into cross-file security review. Existing guardrails often flag risk but recover malicious intent inconsistently under semantics-preserving rewrites. This paper formulates pre-load auditing for untrusted Agent Skills as a robust three-way classification task and introduces SkillGuard-Robust, which combines role-aware evidence extraction, selective semantic verification, and consistency-preserving adjudication. We evaluate SkillGuard-Robust on SkillGuardBench and two public-ecosystem extensions through five large evaluation views ranging from 254 to 404 packages. On the 404-package held-out aggregate, SkillGuard-Robust reaches 97.30% overall exact match, 98.33% malicious-risk recall, and 98.89% attack exact consistency. On the 254-package external-ecosystem view, it reaches 99.66%, 100.00%, and 100.00%, respectively. These results support a bounded conclusion: factorized package auditing materially improves frozen and public-ecosystem robustness, while harsher external-source transfer remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [102] [MGTEVAL: An Interactive Platform for Systemtic Evaluation of Machine-Generated Text Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25152)
*Yuanfan Li,Qi Zhou,Chengzhengxu Li,Zhaohan Zhang,Chenxu Zhao,Zepu Ruan,Chao Shen,Xiaoming Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MGTEVAL是一个用于系统评估机器生成文本检测器的可扩展平台，支持数据集构建、攻击模拟、检测器训练和性能评估四大模块，提供命令行和Web界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MGT检测评估碎片化严重，分散在不同的数据集、预处理方式、攻击方法和评估指标上，导致结果难以比较和复现。

Method: 将工作流分为四个组件：数据集构建（可配置LLM生成MGT）、数据集攻击（支持12种文本攻击）、检测器训练（统一接口）、性能评估（报告有效性、鲁棒性和效率）。平台提供命令行和Web两种交互界面。

Result: 实现了无需重写代码即可进行用户友好实验的评估平台。

Conclusion: MGTEVAL通过标准化和模块化的评估流程，解决了MGT检测领域评估碎片化的问题，提升了结果的可比性和可复现性。

Abstract: We present MGTEVAL, an extensible platform for systematic evaluation of Machine-Generated Text (MGT) detectors. Despite rapid progress in MGT detection, existing evaluations are often fragmented across datasets, preprocessing, attacks, and metrics, making results hard to compare and reproduce. MGTEVAL organizes the workflow into four components: Dataset Building, Dataset Attack, Detector Training, and Performance Evaluation. It supports constructing custom benchmarks by generating MGT with configurable LLMs, applying 12 text attacks to test sets, training detectors via a unified interface, and reporting effectiveness, robustness, and efficiency. The platform provides both command-line and Web-based interfaces for user-friendly experimentation without code rewriting.

</details>


### [103] [AgentDID: Trustless Identity Authentication for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25189)
*Minghui Xu,Xiaoyu Liu,Yihao Guo,Chunchi Liu,Yue Zhang,Xiuzhen Cheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentDID，一个基于去中心化标识符（DIDs）和可验证凭证（VCs）的去中心化框架，用于解决AI agent在自治、跨平台、大规模并发场景下的身份认证与执行状态验证问题。通过挑战-响应机制实现交互时执行条件的动态验证，实验证明其在多agent并发场景下具有可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有身份与访问管理机制针对人类用户或静态机器设计，依赖集中注册、持久标识和稳定执行上下文。然而AI agent的身份具有自管理、短生命周期、与执行状态和能力紧密耦合的特点，现有机制无法满足其身份认证和状态验证需求。

Method: AgentDID利用W3C标准的去中心化标识符（DIDs）和可验证凭证（VCs）实现agent自主管理身份与跨系统认证；引入挑战-响应机制，允许验证者在交互时动态验证agent的执行状态（如上下文和能力是否仍然有效）。

Result: 在W3C标准兼容实现基础上的吞吐量实验表明，AgentDID在多agent并发场景下实现了可扩展的身份认证与状态验证。

Conclusion: AgentDID能有效支持大规模AI agent群体的身份认证与状态验证需求，解决了现有集中式机制在agent自治、动态环境下的局限性。

Abstract: AI agents are autonomous entities that can be instantiated on demand, migrate across platforms, and interact with other agents or services without continuous human supervision. In such environments, identity is critical for establishing reliable interaction semantics among agents that may lack prior trust relationships. However, existing identity and access management mechanisms are designed for human users or static machines, assuming centralized enrollment, persistent identifiers, and stable execution contexts. These assumptions do not hold for AI agents, whose identities are self-managed, short-lived, and tightly coupled with their execution state and capabilities. We study the problem of identity authentication and state verification for AI agents and identify three challenges: (1) supporting self-managed identities for autonomously created agents, (2) enabling authentication under large-scale, concurrent interactions, and (3) verifying agents' dynamic execution state, such as whether their context and capabilities remain valid at interaction time. To address these challenges, we present AgentDID, a decentralized framework for identity authentication and state verification. AgentDID leverages decentralized identifiers (DIDs) and verifiable credentials (VCs), enabling agents to manage their own identities and authenticate across systems without centralized control. To address the limitations of static credential-based approaches, AgentDID introduces a challenge-response mechanism that allows verifiers to validate an agent's execution conditions at interaction time. We implement AgentDID in compliance with W3C standards and evaluate it through throughput experiments with multiple concurrent agents. Results show that the system achieves scalable identity authentication and state verification, demonstrating its potential to support large populations of AI agents.

</details>


### [104] [Secure Conformance Checking using Token-based Replay and Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25190)
*Luis-Armando Rodríguez-Flores,Luciano García-Bañuelos,Abel Armas-Cervantes,Astrid-Monserrat Rivera-Partida*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于同态加密和令牌重放算法的安全合规性检查方法，允许日志拥有者在不对第三方暴露敏感信息的情况下，验证事件日志与过程模型的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的合规性检查要求过程模型和事件日志同时对业务分析师可见，但在实际场景中，日志拥有者可能希望保护日志中的敏感信息，同时仍需检查日志与另一方模型之间的一致性，因此需要一种保护隐私的合规性检查方法。

Method: 基于同态加密和令牌重放算法，通过加密方式在不解密日志数据的情况下执行合规性检查操作。

Result: 使用合成日志进行评估，实验结果表明所提技术具有实用性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种在保护日志敏感信息前提下进行合规性检查的安全方法，并通过实验验证了其可行性。

Abstract: Conformance checking, one of the main process mining operations, aims to identify discrepancies between a process model and an event log. The model represents the expected behaviour, whereas the event log represents the actual process behaviour as captured in information systems' records. Traditionally, the process model and the event log are both accessible to the business analyst performing the conformance checking. However, in some contexts the log's owner may want to protect critical or sensitive information in the log and still check its conformance with respect to a model belonging to another party. In this paper, we propose a secure approach to conformance checking based on the well-known token-based replay algorithm and homomorphic encryption. An evaluation is performed using a synthetic log, showing the practicality of the proposed technique.

</details>


### [105] [Making AI-Assisted Grant Evaluation Auditable without Exposing the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25200)
*Kemal Bicakci*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于TEE（可信执行环境）的架构，通过远程 attestation 使LLM辅助的基金评审过程既可审计、可质疑、可问责，又能防止申请方针对模型和评分标准进行优化作弊。架构包含认证评估包（attested evaluation bundle）和文档规范化/清洗层，并考虑了提示注入风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共机构开始将LLM作为基金评审的决策支持工具，面临治理难题：既不能暴露模型和评分标准让申请方对优化，又必须保证评审过程可审计、可质疑、可问责。现有方案无法同时满足这两方面需求。

Method: 1) 基于TEE的架构，通过远程 attestation 让外部验证者检查所用模型、评分标准、提示模板和输入表征，但不暴露模型权重、专有评分逻辑或中间推理过程；2) 生成认证评估包（attested evaluation bundle）：包含原始提交哈希、规范输入哈希、模型与评分标准度量、评估输出的签名时间戳记录；3) 设计标准化与清洗层（canonicalization and sanitization layer），处理申请者文档中的隐藏或间接指令（提示注入风险），在推理前规范化文档表征并记录可疑变换。

Result: 提出了一种可运行的TEE架构设计，能够生成可外部验证的认证评估包，并包含针对提示注入的防御机制。论文定位了该设计相对于机密AI推理、可审计AI审计、零知识机器学习、算法问责制和AI辅助同行评审等领域的贡献边界。

Conclusion: 远程 attestation 并不能证明评估是公平或科学正确的，但它能使评估过程的一部分变得可外部验证。这是一种审慎的、有明确边界的技术方案。

Abstract: Public agencies are beginning to consider large language models (LLMs) as decision-support tools for grant evaluation. This creates a practical governance problem: the model and scoring rubric should not be exposed in a way that allows applicants to optimize against them, yet the evaluation process must remain auditable, contestable, and accountable.
  We propose a TEE-based architecture that helps reconcile these requirements through remote attestation. The architecture allows an external verifier to check which model, rubric, prompt template, and input representation were used, without exposing model weights, proprietary scoring logic, or intermediate reasoning to applicants or infrastructure operators. The main artifact is an attested evaluation bundle: a signed, timestamped record linking the original submission hash, the canonical input hash, the model-and-rubric measurement, and the evaluation output.
  The paper also considers a scenario-specific prompt injection risk: applicant-controlled documents may contain hidden or indirect instructions intended to influence the LLM evaluator. We therefore include a canonicalization and sanitization layer that normalizes document representations and records suspicious transformations before inference. We position the design relative to confidential AI inference, attestable AI audits, zero-knowledge machine learning, algorithmic accountability, and AI-assisted peer review. The resulting claim is deliberately narrow: remote attestation does not prove that an evaluation is fair or scientifically correct, but it can make part of the evaluation process externally verifiable.

</details>


### [106] [MARD: A Multi-Agent Framework for Robust Android Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25264)
*Xueying Zeng,Youquan Xian,Sihao Liu,Xudong Mou,Yanze Li,Lei Cui,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出MARD，一种基于多智能体框架的Android恶意软件检测方法，通过LLM编排传统静态分析工具，在无需微调的情况下F1达93.46%，单次分析成本低于0.10美元，且对概念漂移和跨域泛化具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习检测模型受概念漂移和浅层特征限制，缺乏深层语义理解和可解释性；LLM虽具备语义推理能力但处理原始代码的token开销过高，且未能充分发挥其在复杂上下文中的深度逻辑推理潜力。

Method: 提出了MARD多智能体框架，将底层确定性分析引擎作为按需执行工具，LLM作为决策编排器，基于ReAct范式设计自主多智能体交互机制，构建高可解释性的定罪证据链。

Result: 无需领域微调，F1达93.46%；单次复杂APK深度分析总成本低于0.10美元；在跨越五年的评估中优于持续学习基线，对概念漂移具有鲁棒性，具备强跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: MARD有效弥合了LLM语义理解与传统静态分析之间的差距，以极低成本实现了高准确率、强可解释性和对概念漂移的鲁棒性，为Android恶意软件检测提供了新的范式。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of Android applications, traditional machine learning-based detection models suffer from concept drift. Additionally, they are constrained by shallow features, lacking deep semantic understanding and interpretability of decisions. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable semantic reasoning capabilities, directly processing massive raw code incurs prohibitive token overhead. Moreover, this approach fails to fully unleash the deep logical reasoning potential of LLMs within complex contexts. To address these limitations, we propose MARD, a multi-agent framework for robust Android malware detection. This framework effectively bridges the gap between the semantic understanding of LLMs and traditional static analysis. It treats underlying deterministic analysis engines as on-demand execution tools, while utilizing the LLM to orchestrate the entire decision-making process. By designing an autonomous multi-agent interaction mechanism based on the ReAct paradigm, MARD constructs a highly interpretable evidentiary chain for conviction. Furthermore, we radically reduce the total cost of conducting a deep analysis of a single complex APK to under $0.10. Evaluations demonstrate that, without any domain-specific fine-tuning, MARD achieves an F1 score of 93.46%. It not only outperforms continual learning baselines but also exhibits robustness against concept drift and strong cross-domain generalization capabilities in evaluations spanning up to five years.

</details>


### [107] [ReTokSync: Self-Synchronizing Tokenization Disambiguation for Generative Linguistic Steganography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25486)
*Yaofei Wang,Rui Wang,Weilong Pang,JiaLiang Han,Yuan Qi,Donghui Hu,Kejiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ReTokSync框架，通过自同步的消歧机制解决生成式语言隐写中的分词歧义问题，仅在歧义实际发生时触发重置，将全局失同步限制为稀疏残差比特错误，在保持安全性、文本质量和嵌入容量的同时实现99.7%以上的提取准确率，并进一步开发双通道机制实现100%端到端恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式语言隐写在部署时面临令牌化歧义问题：同一表面文本在接收端可能被重新分词为不同令牌序列，导致编解码状态不一致，单个局部不匹配会传播为完全提取失败。现有方法或删除歧义令牌（破坏生成分布、影响安全性），或保留分布但大幅降低嵌入容量或带来过高运行时开销。

Method: 提出ReTokSync（重新令牌化同步）框架，一种自同步消歧机制：在生成过程中监测接收端视角的令牌化状态，仅当歧义实际发生时触发纠正性重置。将分词歧义的影响从全局失同步限制为稀疏的残差比特错误，不受影响的歧义无关位置完全保持原状，且与底层隐写算法兼容。

Result: 在英文和中文设置下的实验表明：ReTokSync在分布安全（零KL散度）、文本质量、嵌入容量和运行时方面最接近隐写基线，同时实现99.7%以上的提取准确率。在此基础上进一步开发双通道隐蔽通信机制（ReTokSync为主通道+可靠辅助通道纠正残差），在所有评估配置下实现100%端到端恢复。

Conclusion: ReTokSync有效解决了生成式语言隐写部署中的令牌化歧义问题，在不牺牲安全性、文本质量和效率的前提下实现了极高的提取准确率，双通道机制可完全消除残差错误，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generative linguistic steganography (GLS) enables covert communication by embedding secret messages into the natural language generation process. In practical deployment, however, GLS is vulnerable to tokenization ambiguity: the same surface text may be re-tokenized into a different token sequence at the receiver, breaking the shared decoding state between the communicating parties so that a single local mismatch can propagate into complete extraction failure. Existing solutions either remove ambiguous tokens -- distorting the generation distribution and compromising security -- or preserve the distribution at the cost of substantially reduced embedding capacity or prohibitive runtime overhead. To address this issue, we propose ReTokSync (Re-Tokenization Synchronization), a self-synchronizing disambiguation framework that monitors the receiver-view tokenization during generation and triggers a corrective reset only when ambiguity actually occurs. By confining the effect of tokenization ambiguity to sparse residual bit errors rather than global desynchronization, ReTokSync leaves ambiguity-free positions entirely untouched and remains compatible with the underlying steganographic algorithm. Experiments on both English and Chinese settings show that ReTokSync stays closest to the steganographic baseline in distributional security (zero KL divergence), text quality, embedding capacity, and runtime, while achieving extraction accuracy above 99.7\%. Building on this property, we further develop a two-channel covert communication mechanism in which ReTokSync serves as the primary channel and a reliable auxiliary channel corrects the remaining errors, achieving 100\% end-to-end recovery across all evaluated configurations.

</details>


### [108] [Medoid Prototype Alignment for Cross-Plant Unknown Attack Detection in Industrial Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25544)
*Luyao Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Medoid原型对齐的跨工厂未知攻击检测框架，通过将异构流量压缩到可比较的表征空间，提取稳健的Medoid原型并设计原型校准迁移目标，在天然气和水存储控制系统上实现了平均准确率0.843和平均F1-score 0.838，有效解决了工业控制系统入侵检测中的跨域迁移和未知攻击检测难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有入侵检测器在跨工业工厂部署时面临三大挑战：ICS流量高度依赖站点环境、标签稀缺、部署后常出现未知攻击。直接对齐源域和目标域样本会导致噪声匹配和不稳定迁移，亟需更稳健的跨域对齐方法。

Method: 首先将异构流量压缩到可比较的表征空间，然后提取能够概括每个域局部操作结构的稳健Medoid原型，最后设计原型校准迁移目标：对齐目标原型与源原型，同时保留源域判别性并鼓励目标域的高置信度预测。

Result: 在天然气和水存储控制系统上的四个未知攻击迁移任务中，该方法达到平均准确率0.843和平均F1-score 0.838，优于所有对比模型。实验还发现源-目标方向间存在明显的迁移不对称性，并证实原型引导在具有挑战性的反向迁移设置中尤其有效。

Conclusion: Medoid原型对齐是一种在域偏移条件下实现稳健工业入侵检测的实用解决方案，能够有效减少跨域噪声匹配并提高迁移稳定性。

Abstract: Deploying an intrusion detector trained in one industrial plant to another remains difficult because Industrial Control System (ICS) traffic is highly site-dependent, labels are scarce, and unseen attacks often appear after deployment. To address this challenge, this paper introduces a medoid prototype alignment framework for cross-plant unknown attack detection. Instead of aligning all source and target samples directly, the method first compresses heterogeneous traffic into a comparable representation space and then extracts robust medoid prototypes that summarize local operational structure in each domain. A prototype-calibrated transfer objective is further designed to align target prototypes with source prototypes while preserving source-domain discrimination and encouraging confident target predictions. This strategy reduces noisy cross-domain matching and improves transfer stability under heterogeneous industrial conditions. Experiments conducted on natural gas and water storage control systems show that the proposed method achieves the best average performance among all compared models, reaching an average accuracy of 0.843 and an average F1-score of 0.838 across four unknown-attack transfer tasks. The analysis also shows clear transfer asymmetry between source-target directions and confirms that prototype guidance is especially helpful on challenging reverse-transfer settings. These findings suggest that medoid prototype alignment is a practical solution for robust industrial intrusion detection under domain shift.

</details>


### [109] [SnapGuard: Lightweight Prompt Injection Detection for Screenshot-Based Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25562)
*Mengyao Du,Han Fang,Haokai Ma,Jiahao Chen,Kai Xu,Quanjun Yin,Ee-Chien Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SnapGuard，一种轻量级的截图级Web智能体提示注入攻击检测方法。它通过视觉稳定性指标和对比极性反转文本信号进行多模态特征分析，在8种攻击场景下达到0.75的F1分数，比GPT-4o快8倍且无额外内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
