Motivation: 基于截图的Web智能体容易受到提示注入攻击，而现有的文本防御方法无效，多模态检测方法虽有效但依赖大型视觉语言模型（VLM）带来巨大的计算开销（推理时间慢、GPU内存占用高）。因此，本文探索能否以轻量级方式从截图中检测提示注入攻击。

Method: 提出SnapGuard，将提示注入检测重新定义为网页截图的模态表示分析。利用两种互补信号：(1) 视觉稳定性指标——检测由恶意内容引起的异常平滑梯度分布；(2) 行为导向的文本信号——通过对比极性反转恢复。该方法无需大型VLM，实现轻量级检测。

Result: 在8种攻击设置和2种良性设置下的广泛评估表明，SnapGuard达到0.75的F1分数，优于GPT-4o-prompt，速度提升8倍（1.81秒 vs 14.50秒），且不引入额外内存开销。

Conclusion: 网页截图中的提示注入攻击可以通过轻量级的多模态表征分析有效检测，无需依赖大型视觉语言模型，为Web智能体的安全性提供了一种高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Web agents have emerged as an effective paradigm for automating interactions with complex web environments, yet remain vulnerable to prompt injection attacks that embed malicious instructions into webpage content to induce unintended actions. This threat is further amplified for screenshot-based web agents, which operate on rendered visual webpages rather than structured textual representations, making predominant text-centric defenses ineffective. Although multimodal detection methods have been explored, they often rely on large vision-language models (VLMs), incurring significant computational overhead. The bottleneck lies in the complexity of modern webpages: VLMs must comprehend the global semantics of an entire page, resulting in substantial inference time and GPU memory usage. This raises a critical question: can we detect prompt injection attacks from screenshots in a lightweight manner? In this paper, we observe that injected webpages exhibit distinct characteristics compared to benign ones from both visual and textual perspectives. Building on this insight, we propose SnapGuard, a lightweight yet accurate method that reformulates prompt injection detection as multimodal representation analysis over webpage screenshots. SnapGuard leverages two complementary signals: a visual stability indicator that identifies abnormally smooth gradient distributions induced by malicious content, and action-oriented textual signals recovered via contrast-polarity reversal. Extensive evaluations across eight attacks and two benign settings demonstrate that SnapGuard achieves an F1 score of 0.75, outperforming GPT-4o-prompt while being 8x faster (1.81s vs. 14.50s) and introducing no additional memory overhead.

</details>


### [110] [The Surprising Universality of LLM Outputs: A Real-Time Verification Primitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25634)
*Alex Bogdan,Adrian de Valois-Franklin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现前沿LLM的输出遵循共同的曼德尔布罗特排序分布（两参数模型），并基于此开发了一个极低延迟（每个token 2.6微秒）的CPU评分原语，可用于模型指纹识别和黑盒输出评估。该原语比现有基于采样的检测器快五个数量级，且无需密码学水印或模型内部访问权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM输出检测方法（如基于采样的检测器）延迟极高，无法满足大规模部署需求。同时缺乏轻量级的模型指纹识别手段来验证输出来源，且黑盒场景下的输出质量评估也缺乏高效的参考分布。

Method: 通过对六个前沿模型、两个生成规模和五个保留域的输出进行统计分析，发现token秩-频率分布收敛到两参数曼德尔布罗特分布。利用该分布拟合参数作为模型指纹，并基于此设计一个单趟评分原语，可与log概率组合使用，在API受限时退化为仅秩模式。

Result: 34/36的模型-域拟合R²>0.94，35/36的AIC支持曼德尔布罗特分布优于Zipf。跨模型q值范围(1.63-3.69)远超自举标准差(0.03-0.10)，实现数十个标准差的分离。评分原语延迟仅2.6微秒/token，比现有方法快10万倍。

Conclusion: 该评分原语可作为复合评估栈中的首层分诊工具，在词汇异常和不受支持实体检测方面有效，但在领域适当词汇中的推理错误检测方面存在结构性局限。不能替代基于采样或源条件的验证器。

Abstract: We report a striking statistical regularity in frontier LLM outputs that enables a CPU-only scoring primitive running at 2.6 microseconds per token, with estimated latency up to 100,000$\times$ (five orders of magnitude) below existing sampling-based detectors. Across six contemporary models from five independent vendors, two generation sizes, and five held-out domains, token rank-frequency distributions converge to the same two-parameter Mandelbrot ranking distribution, with 34 of 36 model-by-domain fits exceeding $R^{2} = 0.94$ and 35 of 36 favoring Mandelbrot over Zipf by AIC. The shared family does not collapse the models into statistical duplicates. Fitted Mandelbrot parameters remain cleanly separable between models: the cross-model spread in $q$ (1.63 to 3.69) exceeds its per-model bootstrap standard deviation (0.03 to 0.10) by more than an order of magnitude, yielding tens of standard deviations of separation per few thousand output tokens. Two capabilities follow. First, statistical model fingerprinting: text from a vendor-delivered LLM can be tested against its claimed model family without cryptographic watermarks or access to model internals, supporting provenance verification and silent-substitution audits. Second, a model-agnostic reference distribution for black-box output assessment, from which we derive a single-pass scoring primitive that composes with model log probabilities when available and degrades to a rank-only mode usable on closed APIs. Pilot results on FRANK, TruthfulQA, and HaluEval map where the primitive helps (lexical anomalies, unsupported entities) and where it structurally cannot (reasoning errors in domain-appropriate vocabulary). We position the primitive as a first-pass triage layer in compound evaluation stacks, not as a replacement for sampling-based or source-conditioned verifiers.

</details>


### [111] [Prime-Field PINI: Machine-Checked Composition Theorems for Post-Quantum NTT Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25878)
*Ray Iskander,Khaled Kirah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个关于质数域上算术掩码的组合定理的机器验证证明，核心见解是“更新论证”：在流水线阶段之间施加新鲜随机掩码可使中间导线完美均匀。在Lean 4中完成了18个机器验证证明，并形式化诊断了Microsoft Adams Bridge PQC加速器中缺少级间新鲜掩码的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布尔掩码组合理论（NI、SNI、PINI）已成熟，但后量子密码中基于NTT的算术掩码（在质数域$\mathbb{Z}_q$上）缺乏相应的组合理论，这阻碍了对掩码NTT硬件的形式化安全验证。

Method: 提出“更新论证”：在流水线两级之间施加新鲜随机掩码后，中间导线完全均匀，与第一级安全参数无关。基于此证明PF-PINI组合定理，并在Lean 4中完成形式化验证。同时形式化桥接了Barrett归约的代数模型与硬件忠实算术模型。

Result: 证明了两个PF-PINI gadget组合后满足PF-PINI($k_2$)，第一级的重数被完全擦除；若无新鲜掩码，中间导线重数可达$k_1$。在Lean 4中完成18个机器验证证明，零sorry存根。形式化诊断了Microsoft Adams Bridge加速器缺少级间新鲜掩码的架构缺陷。

Conclusion: 新鲜掩码对于算术掩码组合安全性至关重要；1-Bit Barrier对Barrett和Montgomery归约具有普适性；形式化验证工具（Lean 4）可有效发现后量子密码硬件中的真实安全漏洞。

Abstract: This is Paper 6 of a series of formally-verified analyses of masked NTT hardware for post-quantum cryptography; Paper 1 [1] established structural dependency analysis of the QANARY platform, and Paper 2 [2] quantified security margins under partial NTT masking. Boolean masking composition is well-understood through NI, SNI, and PINI. Arithmetic masking over $\mathbb{Z}_q$ for prime $q$, the foundation of NTT-based post-quantum cryptography, has lacked an analogous theory. We prove, to our knowledge, the first machine-checked composition theorems for arithmetic masking over prime fields. Our key insight is the renewal argument: when a fresh random mask is applied between two pipeline stages, the intermediate wire becomes perfectly uniform regardless of Stage 1's security parameter. For two PF-PINI gadgets with parameters $k_1$ and $k_2$, the composed two-stage pipeline with fresh masking satisfies PF-PINI($k_2$), Stage 1's multiplicity is completely erased from the composed output. Without fresh masking, intermediate wires have multiplicity up to $k_1$, creating a necessary condition for differential power analysis. We formalize both theorems in Lean 4 with 18 machine-checked proofs and zero sorry stubs. We formally bridge the algebraic and hardware-faithful arithmetic models of Barrett reduction, and instantiate the theorems to formally diagnose Microsoft's Adams Bridge PQC accelerator: its absence of fresh inter-stage masking leaves Barrett output wires non-uniform under the first-order probing model, the same architectural flaw that two independent empirical analyses [3, 4] and our own prior structural analysis [1] identified. Computational evidence further suggests the 1-Bit Barrier is universal across Barrett and Montgomery reductions.

</details>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Elite-Driven Support Vector Machines for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25158)
*Mohammad Jafari Jozani,Bahram Moeinianfar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出精英驱动支持向量机(EDSVM)框架，通过在支持向量机上引导精英观测点的松弛变量，实现对参考模型的结构化偏好编码，无需特权特征或全局函数惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SVM纯数据驱动，无法直接编码可信基准模型或对选定数据子集的结构化偏好。

Method: 在正则化经验风险最小化中，对精英观测点(参考SVM的支持向量)的松弛变量添加偏差惩罚，使其向基准松弛值收缩；基于铰链损失和平方松弛损失开发C-EDSVM和LS-EDSVM两种具体模型，推导对偶二次规划并给出分类校准条件。

Result: EDSVM能紧密追踪参考SVM的行为模式，在UCI基准测试中预测性能与C-SVM、LINEX-SVM、LS-SVM相当或更优。

Conclusion: EDSVM提供了一种局部化、边界对齐的方式来编码模型偏好，是对知识蒸馏和SVM+/LUPI方法的有效替代。

Abstract: Support vector machines (SVMs) are a standard tool for binary classification, but their classical formulations are purely data-driven and offer no direct way to encode trusted benchmark models or structured preferences on selected subsets of the data. We propose Elite-Driven Support Vector Machines (EDSVM), a general framework that augments regularized empirical risk minimization by guiding the slack variables for a curated set of elite observations (typically the union of support vectors from one or more reference SVMs). EDSVM combines the usual slack loss with a deviation penalty that shrinks new slacks toward benchmark slack values, defining a localized, margin-aligned notion of proximity to reference models, unlike global function penalties in knowledge distillation or teacher-student methods, and without requiring privileged features as in SVM+/LUPI. Within this framework we develop two concrete models, C-EDSVM and LS-EDSVM, based respectively on hinge-type and squared-slack losses. For both variants we derive dual quadratic programs that can be implemented with modest modifications of standard SVM solvers, and we give simple sufficient conditions under which the induced margin losses are classification calibrated. Simulation studies and experiments on several UCI benchmarks show that EDSVMs closely track the behaviour induced by reference SVMs while achieving predictive performance that is competitive with, and sometimes better than, C-SVM, LINEX-SVM, and LS-SVM.

</details>


### [113] [A Finite Time Analysis of Thompson Sampling for Bayesian Optimization with Preferential Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25025)
*Joseph Lazzaro,Davide Buffelli,Da-shan Shiu,Sattar Vakili*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于汤普森采样（TS）的贝叶斯优化方法，用于处理偏好反馈（成对比较而非标量分数），通过单调链接函数对潜在效用差异建模，并利用基础核诱导的对偶核。文章提供了有限时间分析，证明该方法在性能上与标准标量反馈贝叶斯优化的TS方法匹配，并展示了合成数据与真实世界示例上的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好反馈（成对比较）在人类、实验室、专家参与的设计以及科学发现中应用日益广泛，但现有的贝叶斯优化方法主要针对标量反馈，缺乏对偏好反馈的高效处理方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于汤普森采样（TS）的贝叶斯优化方法，使用单调链接函数对潜在效用差异建模，通过基础核诱导对偶核来处理偏好数据，并设计了双TS配对变体。

Result: 有限时间分析表明该方法性能与标准标量反馈贝叶斯优化的TS方法相匹配，并在合成数据和真实世界示例上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的偏好反馈贝叶斯优化方法在理论上保证了与标量反馈方法相当的收敛性能，实践中也表现良好，为偏好反馈场景下的优化提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Preference feedback, in the form of pairwise comparisons rather than scalar scores, has seen increasing use in applications such as human-, laboratory-, and expert-in-the-loop design, as well as scientific discovery. We propose a Thompson Sampling (TS) approach to Bayesian optimization with preferential feedback that models comparisons using a monotone link on latent utility differences and leverages the dueling kernel induced by a base kernel. We provide a finite-time analysis showing that the performance of the proposed method matches that of standard TS for conventional Bayesian optimization with scalar feedback. The analysis exploits the anchor invariance of TS for challenger selection and introduces a double-TS pairing variant. We also demonstrate the performance of the method on both synthetic and real-world examples.

</details>


### [114] [Online learning with Erdős-Rényi side-observation graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25271)
*Tomáš Kocák,Gergely Neu,Michal Valko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗性多臂老虎机问题中，允许学习器以固定但未知的概率r观测非选择臂损失的情况，提出了两种适用于不同r范围的算法，并给出了相应的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对抗性多臂老虎机问题中，传统设定下学习器只能观测到所选臂的损失，但实际场景中可能以一定概率获得其他臂的部分反馈信息。本文旨在利用这种部分边信息来提升学习性能。

Method: 提出两种算法：第一种适用于$r \ge (\log T)/(2N)$，遗憾界为$O(\sqrt{(T/r) \log N})$；第二种适用于更小的r，遗憾界为$O(\sqrt{(T/r) \log (N+T)})$。此外还给出了一种快速估计程序来判断r的范围。

Result: 两种算法分别在不同r范围内取得了与知晓r的最优算法仅差对数因子的遗憾界。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了在部分反馈可观测的对抗性多臂老虎机问题中，即使观测概率r未知，仍可设计出接近最优性能的算法。

Abstract: We consider adversarial multi-armed bandit problems where the learner is allowed to observe losses of a number of arms beside the arm that it actually chose. We study the case where all non-chosen arms reveal their loss with a fixed but unknown probability $r$, independently of each other and the action of the learner. We propose two algorithms that work for different ranges of $r$. We show that after $T$ rounds in a bandit problem with $N$ arms, the expected regret of our first algorithm is $O(\sqrt{(T /r) \log N })$ whenever $r\ge(\log T)/(2N)$, while our second algorithm achieves a regret of $O(\sqrt{(T/r) \log (N+T)})$ for smaller values of $r$. We also give a quick estimation procedure that decides the range of~$r$. All our bounds are within logarithmic factors of the best achievable performance of any algorithm that is even allowed to know~$r$.

</details>


### [115] [Spectral bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25272)
*Tomáš Kocák,Rémi Munos,Branislav Kveton,Shipra Agrawal,Michal Valko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了图上赌博机问题，其中臂的收益在图上平滑。提出了有效维度的概念，并设计了三种在此维度上呈线性和次线性缩放的算法。实验表明，仅需数十次节点评估即可学习数千个项目的用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图上的平滑函数在流形学习和半监督学习中有广泛应用，但现有的在线学习框架未充分利用图结构信息，导致累积遗憾随节点数量增加而大幅增长。本文旨在解决如何利用图平滑性进行高效在线学习的问题。

Method: 引入了有效维度的概念（该维度在真实世界图中较小），并提出了三种算法，其累积遗憾在该有效维度上呈线性和次线性缩放。这些算法利用图上收益的平滑性来指导推荐决策。

Result: 在内容推荐问题上，仅需数十次节点评估即可学习数千个项目的用户偏好估计器，实现了高效的图结构赌博机学习。

Conclusion: 利用图结构平滑性的赌博机方法能够显著降低样本复杂度，使得大规模推荐系统可以在少量交互中完成有效学习。

Abstract: Smooth functions on graphs have wide applications in manifold and semi-supervised learning. In this work, we study a bandit problem where the payoffs of arms are smooth on a graph. This framework is suitable for solving online learning problems that involve graphs, such as content-based recommendation. In this problem, each item we can recommend is a node of an undirected graph and its expected rating is similar to the one of its neighbors. The goal is to recommend items that have high expected ratings. We aim for the algorithms where the cumulative regret with respect to the optimal policy would not scale poorly with the number of nodes. In particular, we introduce the notion of an effective dimension, which is small in real-world graphs, and propose three algorithms for solving our problem that scale linearly and sublinearly in this dimension. Our experiments on content recommendation problem show that a good estimator of user preferences for thousands of items can be learned from just tens of node evaluations.

</details>


### [116] [Deflation-Free Optimal Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25664)
*Sharmin Afroz,Brendan Ames*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种无放缩的稀疏最优评分方法(DFSOS)，通过同时估计所有判别向量并在全局正交约束下优化，避免了传统放缩策略的误差传播问题，在高维分类任务中取得了与现有方法相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏最优评分(SOS)方法采用基于放缩(deflation)的策略顺序计算判别向量，该策略会传播误差并产生次优解，因此需要一种能够同时估计所有判别向量的方法。

Method: 将Bregman迭代与正交约束优化相结合，将原问题分解为评分向量、判别向量和正交性强制三个可处理的子问题，在显式全局正交约束下同时估计所有判别向量。

Result: 在合成数据和真实时间序列数据上的大量实验表明，DFSOS的分类准确率与现有基于放缩的方法相当或更优，且具备收敛性保证。

Conclusion: 无放缩方法为高维问题中的稀疏判别分析提供了一种鲁棒且有效的框架。

Abstract: Sparse Optimal Scoring (SOS) reformulates linear discriminant analysis to enable feature selection through elastic net regularization, making it well-suited for high-dimensional settings where the number of features exceeds observations. Most existing SOS methods use deflation-based strategies that compute discriminant vectors sequentially, which can propagate errors and produce suboptimal solutions. We propose a novel approach that estimates all discriminant vectors simultaneously under an explicit global orthogonality constraint, which we call Deflation-Free Sparse Optimal Scoring (DFSOS). DFSOS combines Bregman iteration with orthogonality-constrained optimization, decomposing the problem into tractable subproblems for scoring vectors, discriminant vectors, and orthogonality enforcement. We establish convergence to stationary points of the augmented Lagrangian under mild conditions. Extensive experiments using synthetic data and real-world time series data demonstrate that DFSOS achieves classification accuracy comparable to or better than existing deflation-based methods. These results indicate that deflation-free approaches offer a robust and effective framework for sparse discriminant analysis in high-dimensional problems.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [The optimal betting wealth growth rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25280)
*Ashwin Ram,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文刻画了在Kelly博弈中对一般i.i.d.零假设$\mathscr{P}$反复下注时财富的最优增长率，证明其等于$\lim_{n \to \infty}n^{-1}\inf_{P \in (\mathscr P)^n)^{\circ\circ}} \mathrm{KL}(Q^n,P)$，并揭示该量通常小于文献中常用的$\mathrm{KL}_{\inf}(Q,\mathscr{P})$。当$\mathrm{KL}_{\mathrm{inf}}(\cdot,\mathscr P)$在$Q$处弱下半连续时两者相等。论文还提供了简单备择假设下power-one序贯检验存在性的首个充要条件，并推导了针对复合$\mathscr Q$的最优最坏情况增长率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Larsson等人关于numeraire的最新结果推广到序贯设定，刻画Kelly博弈中财富增长率的最优界，并澄清该界与经典KL散度下确界之间的关系。

Method: 利用bipolar（双极）理论刻画可行赌注集合，通过KL散度的几何性质分析增长率；引入弱下半连续性条件建立两个量的等价关系；基于约化滤子的检验超鞅构造最优序贯检验。

Result: 得到了财富最优增长率的精确表达式，证明其一般小于$\mathrm{KL}_{\inf}(Q,\mathscr{P})$；给出了两者相等的充分条件（弱下半连续/弱紧性）；建立了power-one序贯检验存在性的充要条件；证明了检验超鞅足以处理所有i.i.d.检验问题。

Conclusion: 完整推广了Larsson等人的numeraire结果到序贯设定，统一了Kelly博弈中财富增长与序贯假设检验的理论框架。

Abstract: This paper characterizes the best possible rate of growth of wealth in a Kelly betting game when repeatedly betting against a general i.i.d. null hypothesis $\mathscr{P}$, but the data are drawn i.i.d from an arbitrary alternative $Q$. We prove that it equals $\lim_{n \to \infty}n^{-1}\inf_{P \in (\mathscr P)^n)^{\circ\circ}} \mathrm{KL}(Q^n,P)$, where ${\mathscr P}^n = \{P^n: P \in \mathscr{P}\}$ and $(\mathscr {P}^n)^{\circ\circ}$ is its bipolar, i.e., this rate is achievable and one cannot do better. This quantity is in general smaller than a more popular quantity in the literature, $\mathrm{KL}_{\inf}(Q,\mathscr{P}) := \inf_{P \in \mathscr P}\mathrm{KL}(Q,P)$. If $\mathrm{KL}_{\mathrm{inf}}(\cdot,\mathscr P)$ is weakly lowersemicontinuous (w.l.s.c.) at $Q$, we show that the two quantities are equal; in particular, this happens when $\mathscr P$ is weakly compact. For simple alternatives, we provide the first matching necessary and sufficient condition for when power-one sequential tests exist (without assumptions on $\mathscr P, Q$). We also derive the optimal worst-case growth rate against composite $\mathscr Q$. We emphasize that test supermartingales on reduced filtrations suffice for all i.i.d. testing problems, and more general e-processes are not required. We thus completely generalize the recent results of Larsson et al.~\cite{larsson2025numeraire} to the sequential setting.

</details>


### [118] [Self-organized regime switching in null-recurrent dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25368)
*Johannes Brutsche,Sebastian Hahn,Angelika Rohde*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对具有两段常数波动率的零常返扩散过程，基于离散观测数据研究了变点ρ的剖面MLE估计的收敛速率和极限分布，证明了其收敛速率为n^{-(1+γ)/2}且达到极小极大最优，极限分布为非标准形式——由双随机漂移Poisson过程的argsup给出，该极限与采样频率无关且对参数连续依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为零常返过程（特别是波动率存在跳跃的随机过程）中变点参数的统计推断提供理论基础，处理低频率采样（包括γ=0时退化为零常返马尔可夫链）情形下的估计问题。

Method: 利用零常返马尔可夫链的长期行为与固定时间区间上的填充渐近性之间的关联，结合强马尔可夫性和耦合论证，并借助在真实参数起点下过程的自相似性。

Result: 剖面MLE的收敛速率为n^{-(1+γ)/2}并达到极小极大最优；极限分布为双随机漂移Poisson过程的argsup，该极限与采样频率γ无关，且对参数ρ, α, β连续依赖。

Conclusion: 所提出的估计方法在零常返扩散变点估计问题上具有最优收敛速率，且极限分布的良好连续性保证了统计推断的可行性。

Abstract: Based on discrete observations $X_0,X_Δ,\dots, X_{nΔ}$ for $Δ=n^{-γ}$ with $γ\in [0,1)$ of the null-recurrent dynamic $dX_t = σ(X_t)dW_t$ with a Brownian motion $W$ and $σ(x)=α\mathbb{1}\{x<ρ\} + β\mathbb{1}\{x\geq ρ\}$, we derive rate of convergence and limiting distribution of the profile MLE for $ρ$. This includes low-frequency asymptotics ($γ=0$) for which the observations form a null-recurrent Markov chain. The derived non-standard limit is the argsup over a doubly stochastic drifted Poisson process explicitly involving the local time of oscillating Brownian motion. Its dependence on $ρ$ as well as the unknown volatility levels $α$ and $β$ is shown to be continuous w.r.t. the topology of weak convergence, enabling statistical inference. Whereas this limit is independent of the sampling frequency, the profile MLE's rate of convergence equals $n^{-(1+γ)/2}$ and is proven to be minimax optimal. The surprising idea of the proof of the limit theorem is to relate the long-term behavior of the null-recurrent Markov chain to the infill asymptotics on a fixed time interval. Indeed, in the very special case that $(X_t)_{t\geq 0}$ is started in the true parameter $X_0=ρ_0$, the process $(X_t-ρ_0)_{t\geq 0}$ is shown to possess a desirable distributional self-similarity. On basis of the strong Markov property, the artificial constallation of starting in $ρ_0$ is finally overcome by a coupling argument.

</details>


### [119] [Sharp adaptive nonparametric testing for constant volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25668)
*Johannes Brutsche,Lukas Riepl*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对非参数高斯白噪声模型，提出了基于离散观测的波动率函数常数性检验方法，证明了该检验具有 minimax-最优性和适应性，并利用隐函数定理构造了最优常数所需的高度参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非参数高斯白噪声模型中，检验波动率函数σ(·)是否为常数是一个基础但未充分解决的问题。现有方法缺乏对检验最优性的理论保证，且需要确定衡量偏离原假设的最佳度量方式。

Method: 基于离散观测构建检验统计量；利用infill渐近框架分析检验的minimax最优性和适应性；通过隐函数定理证明方程F_n(b)=0的可解性并给出解的定量界，从而构造高度为h(b)的备择假设以推导最优常数。

Result: 证明了所提检验是minimax-最优且自适应的；表明偏离常数性原假设的最佳度量是σ(t)与其L²平均的比值；通过隐函数定理成功构造了所需假设并给出了解的定量界。

Conclusion: 建立了波动率常数性检验的最优性理论框架，为后续非参数波动率推断提供了理论基础和方法论支撑。

Abstract: Based on discrete observations, we develop a test to infer if the volatility function $σ(\cdot)$ within the nonparametric Gaussian white noise model $dY_t = σ(t)dW_t$ is constant. The testing procedure is shown to be minimax-optimal and adaptive for infill asymptotics and these results entail that a deviation from the null hypothesis of constancy is best measured in terms of the ratio of $σ(t)$ and its $L^2$-average. The derivation of optimal constants requires the construction of hypotheses with height $h(b)$, where the parameter $b$ solves $F_n(b)=0$ for given functions $F_n$. Proving this equation to be solvable for each $n\in\mathbb{N}$ and establishing quantitative bounds of the solutions is built upon the implicit function theorem.

</details>


### [120] [Implications of weak convergence rates of Markov transition kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25867)
*Austin Brown*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文将马尔可夫转移核的弱收敛界扩展到Lipschitz函数方差的收敛界；在可逆情况下，转移核的弱收敛率可推导出卡方散度收敛界（若初始测度密度为Lipschitz）。这些结果为马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛模拟中Lipschitz函数的中心极限定理提供了新工具，并应用于高维Metropolis-Hastings算法稳定性、随机梯度下降和随机延迟方程解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱收敛界主要关注分布本身的收敛，但缺乏对Lipschitz函数方差收敛的理论保证，而这正是MCMC中心极限定理成立的关键条件。

Method: 通过建立弱收敛速率与Lipschitz函数方差收敛界之间的定量关系，并在可逆马尔可夫链下进一步将弱收敛率转化为卡方散度收敛界。

Result: 获得了马尔可夫转移核对Lipschitz函数方差的收敛界，并在可逆情形下建立了从弱收敛率到卡方散度收敛的桥梁。

Conclusion: 所提理论为证明MCMC中Lipschitz函数的中心极限定理提供了新工具，并在高维Metropolis-Hastings、随机梯度下降和随机延迟方程等场景中展示了应用价值。

Abstract: This article extends weak convergence bounds of Markov transition kernels to convergence bounds on the variance of the Markov kernel applied to Lipschitz functions. In the reversible case, weak convergence rates of the transition kernels imply chi-squared divergence convergence bounds if the density of the initialization measure is Lipschitz. These results provide new tools to establish central limit theorems for Lipschitz functions used in Markov chain Monte Carlo simulations. Applications are explored to the stability of Metropolis-Hastings algorithms in high dimensions, stochastic gradient descent, and solutions to stochastic delay equations.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [GCA-BULF: A Bottom-Up Framework for Short-Term Load Forecasting Using Grouped Critical Appliances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24766)
*Yunhao Yao,Jinwei Fang,Puhan Luo,Zhiqiang Wang,Jiahui Hou,Xiang-Yang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GCA-BULF框架，通过筛选关键电器并分组预测，实现更精准的短期负荷预测，相比现有方法提升20%-92%的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自上而下预测方法难以捕捉混合电器负荷的复杂模式；自下而上方法虽准确但监控所有电器成本高且许多电器对总负荷预测贡献有限。需要一种既能利用电器级数据提升精度、又避免全量监控成本的方法。

Method: GCA-BULF框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 关键电器过滤模块——根据功耗、开关频率、使用周期对电器排序，通过迭代负荷分解识别关键电器；(2) 关联电器分组模块——基于时空相关性对关键电器聚类，进行组级预测；(3) 协同负荷预测模块——融合多组预测结果优化总负荷预测。

Result: 在住宅和办公楼负荷预测任务上，GCA-BULF相比自上而下方法提升20.85%-57.88%，相比自下而上方法提升33.03%-92.48%。

Conclusion: 基于分组关键电器的自下而上预测框架能有效平衡预测精度与监控成本，在短期负荷预测任务上显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: With the rise of time-of-use and tiered electricity pricing, energy consumers are encouraged to adopt peak-shifting strategies by automatically controlling high-power appliances. These help lower energy costs while enhancing the power grid's stability. To support such energy management with high resilience and responsiveness, reliable short-term load forecasting (STLF) plays a critical role. STLF predicts electricity consumption over time horizons ranging from minutes to days, using historical data, temporal patterns, and contextual factors. Traditional top-down forecasting methods struggle to capture the complex consumption patterns of diverse and mixed appliance loads. Although bottom-up methods improve forecasting accuracy by integrating appliance-level data, monitoring all appliances is costly, and many do not meaningfully impact total load prediction. Therefore, we propose GCA-BULF, a bottom-up short-term load forecasting framework based on grouped critical appliances, supported by three key designs. First, the Critical Appliance Filtering module ranks appliances according to their power consumption, switching frequency, and usage pattern periodicity, and identifies critical ones through iterative load decomposition. Next, the Related Appliance Grouping module clusters these appliances based on spatial and temporal correlations for group-level forecasting. Finally, the Collaborative Load Forecasting module refines the total load prediction by combining multiple group-level forecasts. We evaluate GCA-BULF on residential and office building load forecasting tasks. Experimental results reveal that GCA-BULF improves hourly total load forecasting by 20.85%-57.88% compared to existing top-down methods and by 33.03%-92.48% compared to bottom-up methods.

</details>


### [122] [Automated detection of pediatric congenital heart disease from phonocardiograms using deep and handcrafted feature fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24767)
*Abdul Jabbar,Ethan Grooby,Yang Yi Poh,Khawza I. Ahmad,Md Hassanuzzaman,Raqibul Mostafa,Ahsan H. Khandoker,Faezeh Marzbanrad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于深度特征融合的数字化听诊器方法，利用心音记录自动检测先天性心脏病，在751名孟加拉国儿童数据集上达到92%准确率、91%敏感性和特异性、96% AUROC，为低资源环境提供经济有效的筛查工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先天性心脏病是最常见的出生缺陷，影响约1%的新生儿。超声心动图作为金标准诊断方法成本高且难以在低资源环境中普及；同时，专业临床医师数量有限且解读病理模式的能力存在差异，导致诊断延迟和临床医生间/内差异。

Method: 提出基于深度特征融合的方法，融合深度特征与手工特征，利用数字化听诊器采集心音信号实现CHD自动检测。数据来自孟加拉国751名儿科受试者（1个月至16岁），在四个听诊位置（二尖瓣、主动脉瓣、肺动脉瓣、三尖瓣）记录PCG信号，由心脏病专家标注为CHD或非CHD。采用70%训练、20%验证、10%测试的患者级划分。

Result: 模型达到92%准确率、91%敏感性、91%特异性、96% AUROC和92% F1分数。

Conclusion: 该模型有望作为低资源环境中经济有效的远程筛查工具，实现CHD的高效实时检测。

Abstract: Congenital heart disease (CHD) is the most common type of birth defect, impacting about 1% of live births worldwide. Echocardiography, the gold-standard diagnostic method, is costly and inaccessible in low-resource settings. Diagnosis is delayed due to limited skilled experts, whose ability to interpret pathological patterns varies significantly, causing inter- and intra-clinician variability. Therefore, we present a new method for a more accessible diagnostic modality, the digital stethoscope, to detect CHDs. Our method is based on deep feature fusion, integrating deep and handcrafted features for the automated early detection of CHDs. For this work, Phonocardiography (PCG) recordings were obtained from 751 pediatric subjects (Age:1 month- 16 years) in Bangladesh, ranging from infants to adults at four auscultation locations: mitral valve (MV), aortic valve (AV), pulmonary valve (PV), and tricuspid valve (TV). These recordings were labeled based on confirmed diagnoses by cardiologists as either cases of CHD or non-CHD. The results demonstrated that our proposed model achieved an accuracy of 92%, a sensitivity of 91%, and a specificity of 91%, based on a patient-wise split of 70% training, 20% validation, and 10% testing. Furthermore, the Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve (AUROC) of 96%, and an F1-score of 92%. This model promises efficient real-time remote detection of CHDs as a cost-effective screening tool for low-resource settings.

</details>


### [123] [Architecture Determines Observability in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24801)
*Thomas Carmichael*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究自回归Transformer中激活值对输出置信度未暴露的决策质量信号的可观测性。定义可观测性为在中层激活中线性读取每token决策质量的能力（控制最大softmax置信度和激活范数后）。发现可观测性并非Transformer的通用属性：Pythia套件中24层16头配置在所有实验中部分相关系数仅约0.10，而其他六种配置达0.21-0.38；且训练过程中该信号会消退。不同模型系列（如Qwen 2.5与Llama、Mistral与Llama 3.1）间可观测性差异显著。基于WikiText训练的观测器可迁移至下游QA任务，在20%标记率下额外捕获10.9-13.4%的置信度遗漏错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归Transformer会犯高置信度的错误，但激活值监控能在模型内部保留输出置信度未暴露的信号时才有用。而这种保留由架构和训练方式决定，因此需要系统研究不同架构下激活值对决策质量的可观测性差异。

Method: 定义可观测性为控制最大softmax置信度和激活范数后，从中层冻结激活中线性读取每token决策质量（是否正确）的部分相关系数。在Pythia控制套件（13个模型、6个系列）中进行系统比较，包括不同层数、头数配置，以及检查点动态分析。跨独立训练配方（Qwen 2.5、Llama、Mistral）验证现象。

Result: Pythia中24层16头配置在所有实验中部分相关系数仅约0.10，而其他配置达0.21-0.38。该配置的坍塌在训练过程中涌现，早期检查点有信号但训练后期消失。Qwen 2.5与Llama 3B可观测性差异达2.9倍，Mistral 7B保持可观测性而Llama 3.1 8B坍塌。WikiText训练的观测器迁移至下游QA任务，额外捕获10.9-13.4%的置信度遗漏错误。

Conclusion: 可观测性并非Transformer的通用属性，而是由架构（层数、头数）和训练配方共同决定。架构选择本质上是一个监控决策，因为某些架构会消除内部可读的决策质量信号，而另一些则保留。即使在广泛使用的模型系列中也存在显著差异。

Abstract: Autoregressive transformers make confident errors, but activation monitoring can catch them only if the model preserves an internal signal that output confidence does not expose. This preservation is determined by architecture and training recipe. We define observability as the linear readability of per-token decision quality from frozen mid-layer activations after controlling for max-softmax confidence and activation norm. The correction is essential. Confidence controls absorb 57.7% of raw probe signal on average across 13 models in 6 families.
  Observability is not a generic property of transformers. In Pythia's controlled suite, every tested run with the 24-layer, 16-head configuration collapses to rho_partial ~0.10 across a 3.5x parameter gap and two Pile variants, while six other configurations occupy a separated healthy band from 0.21 to 0.38. The output-controlled residual collapses at the same points, and neither tested nonlinear probes nor layer sweeps recover healthy-range signal. Checkpoint dynamics show the collapse is emergent during training. Both configurations at matched hidden dimension form the signal at the earliest measured checkpoint, but training erases it in the (24L, 16H) class while predictive loss continues improving.
  Across independent recipes the collapse map changes but the phenomenon persists. Qwen 2.5 and Llama differ by 2.9x at matched 3B scale with probe seed distributions that do not overlap, while Mistral 7B preserves observability where Llama 3.1 8B collapses despite similar broad architecture. A WikiText-trained observer transfers to downstream QA without training on those tasks, catching errors confidence misses. At 20% flag rate, its exclusive catch rate is 10.9-13.4% of all errors in seven of nine model-task cells. Architecture selection is a monitoring decision.

</details>


### [124] [Intrinsic Mutual Information as a Modulator for Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24804)
*Peng Liao,Peijia Zheng,Lingbo Li,Shangsong Liang,Lin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RMiPO框架，利用响应级互信息进行离线偏好优化，在减少超参数调优的同时（训练开销降低15%以上）实现优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线偏好优化方法（如DPO）虽能对齐人类价值观，但需要大量超参数调优导致时间开销大，且已有改进方法未能完全消除对超参数调优的依赖。

Method: 提出RMiPO框架，利用响应级互信息（Response-level Mutual information）进行偏好优化，通过超参数调制动态解耦偏好贡献，仅增加极小的计算开销。

Result: 实验结果表明RMiPO在性能上持续优于现有方法，同时训练开销降低超过15%。

Conclusion: RMiPO是一种轻量高效的离线偏好优化框架，有效解决了超参数调优负担问题，实现了性能提升与训练效率的平衡。

Abstract: Offline preference optimization methods, such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), offer significant advantages in aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with human values. However, achieving optimal performance with these methods typically involves additional hyperparameter tuning, resulting in substantial time overhead. Although prior work has proposed a range of improvements, these methods remain limited in effectiveness and have not fully eliminated reliance on hyperparameter tuning. In this work, we propose RMiPO, a lightweight and efficient framework for offline preference optimization. RMiPO leverages intrinsic Response-level Mutual information for Preference Optimization with hyperparameter modulation, dynamically decoupling preference contributions at negligible additional computational cost. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that RMiPO achieves consistently superior performance over existing methods while reducing training overhead by more than 15\%. Our code is available at https://github.com/liavonpenn/rmipo.

</details>


### [125] [minAction.net: Energy-First Neural Architecture Design -- From Biological Principles to Systematic Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24805)
*Martin G. Frasch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一篇涵盖2203个实验的论文，验证了能量感知学习：架构单独解释的精度方差极小（partial eta²=0.001），架构×数据集交互效应显著（partial eta²=0.44）；单参数能量正则化目标函数可将内部激活能量降至基线6%且不损失精度；基于作用量原理的能量优先架构在模态内训练效率提升5-33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习优化精度时未考虑内部计算成本，而物理与生物系统受内在能量约束，因此需要评估能量感知学习。

Method: 在视觉、文本、神经形态和生理数据集上进行2203个实验，每配置10个种子，采用因子统计分析。提出能量正则化目标L = L_CE + λ·E(θ,x)，四阶量级λ扫描验证；提出基于作用量原理的能量优先架构。

Result: 架构单独解释方差极小（partial eta²=0.001），架构×数据集交互显著（partial eta²=0.44）；中等λ下内部激活能量降至基线6%且MNIST无精度损失；能量优先架构带来5-33%训练效率提升。

Conclusion: 提出了将作用量泛函、自由能和KL正则化目标之间的结构对应视为设计假设而非推导，为能量感知学习提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Modern machine learning optimizes for accuracy without explicitly accounting for internal computational cost, even though physical and biological systems operate under intrinsic energy constraints. We evaluate energy-aware learning across 2,203 experiments spanning vision, text, neuromorphic, and physiological datasets, using 10 seeds per configuration and performing a factorial statistical analysis. Three findings emerge. First, architecture alone explains negligible variance in accuracy (partial eta^2 = 0.001). In contrast, the architecture x dataset interaction is large (partial eta^2 = 0.44, p < 0.001), demonstrating that optimal architecture depends critically on task modality and rejecting the assumption of a universal best architecture. Second, a controlled lambda-sweep over four orders of magnitude validates a single-parameter energy-regularized objective L = L_CE + lambda * E(theta, x): internal activation energy decreases to 6% of baseline at moderate lambda with no accuracy degradation on MNIST. Third, energy-first architectures inspired by an action-principle framework yield 5-33% within-modality training-efficiency gains over conventional baselines. These results emerge from a research program that interprets learning through a structural correspondence between the action functional in classical mechanics, free energy in statistical physics, and KL-regularized objectives in variational inference. We frame this correspondence as a design hypothesis rather than a derivation.

</details>


### [126] [Nautile-370M: Spectral Memory Meets Attention in a Small Reasoning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24809)
*Maixent Chenebaux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Nautile-370M是一个3.71亿参数的小语言模型，采用混合骨干架构（两个SeqCond Attention层与一个Transformer层交替），在严格参数和推理预算下实现高效推理。模型在TPU v4-64和NVIDIA DGX Spark上训练，理论证明了SCA读出机制在连续极限下至少与全自注意力表达力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格参数和推理预算约束下，设计一个既能保持长序列高效性和状态追踪能力，又能保留注意力机制表达力的小型语言模型。

Method: 采用混合骨干架构：两个SeqCond Attention (SCA)层（基于SeqCondenser的线性时间谱序列算子）与一个Transformer层交替堆叠。SCA读出机制被证明可精确检索前缀摘要中的任意token，且在连续极限下可复现softmax注意力的任意输出。

Result: 理论证明SCA读出机制在连续极限下至少与全自注意力具有相同的表达力，同时保持线性时间复杂度的长上下文效率。

Conclusion: Nautile-370M在严格参数和推理预算下实现了高效推理，其混合架构在表达力和效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: We present Nautile-370M, a 371-million-parameter small language model designed for efficient reasoning under strict parameter and inference budgets. Nautile-370M uses a hybrid backbone in which two SeqCond Attention (SCA) layers, a linear-time spectral sequence operator inspired by SeqCondenser, alternate with one transformer layer. This design aims to retain the long-context efficiency and state-tracking benefits of structured sequential models while preserving the expressive token-to-token routing of attention. The model was trained on a single Cloud TPU v4-64 pod slice provided through the Google TPU Research Cloud (TRC) program; the subsequent reinforcement learning stage was carried out on a single NVIDIA DGX Spark. We prove that the SCA readout mechanism can exactly retrieve any individual token from the prefix summary and can reproduce any output of softmax attention as a special case, establishing that SCA is at least as expressive as full self-attention in the continuous limit. We also describe the training data pipeline and outline a reinforcement learning stage specialized for reasoning, verification, and response quality.

</details>


### [127] [Time-varying Interaction Graph ODE for Dynamic Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24811)
*Xiaoyi Wang,Zhiqiang Wang,Jianqing Liang,Xingwang Zhao,Chuangyin Dang,Zhen Jin,Jiye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TI-ODE提出将图神经ODE的演化函数分解为一组可学习的交互基函数，并通过时间依赖的可学习权重动态组合，以建模动态图中节点交互模式的多样性和时变性，在六个动态图数据集上取得SOTA性能，并展现出更强的鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经ODE在动态图场景中使用统一的消息传递机制，假设节点间交互在任何时刻共享相同的消息传递函数，难以捕捉交互模式的多样性和时变性。

Method: 将图ODE的演化函数分解为一组可学习的交互基函数，每个基函数对应一种不同类型的节点间交互，通过时间依赖的可学习权重动态组合这些基函数，使交互模式能够随时间自适应演化。

Result: 在六个动态图数据集上，TI-ODE在属性预测任务中持续优于现有方法并达到SOTA；在Covid数据集上验证了可解释性和泛化性；理论与实验均证明其比统一消息传递机制的模型具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TI-ODE通过可学习的时变交互基函数有效建模了动态图中节点交互模式的多样性和时变性，在性能、鲁棒性和可解释性方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Graph neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ODE) combine neural ODE with the message passing mechanism of Graph Neural Networks (GNN), providing a continuous-time modeling method for graph representation learning. However, in dynamic graph scenarios, existing graph neural ODEs typically employ a unified message passing mechanism, assuming that inter-node interactions share the same message passing function at any time, which makes it challenging to capture the diversity and time-varying nature of inter-node interaction patterns. To address this, we propose Time-varying Interaction Graph Ordinary Differential Equations (TI-ODE). The core idea of TI-ODE is to decompose the evolution function of a graph ODE into a set of learnable interaction basis functions, where each basis function corresponds to a distinct type of inter-node interaction. These basis functions are dynamically combined through time-dependent learnable weights, enabling inter-node interaction patterns to adaptively evolve over time. Experimental results on six dynamic graph datasets demonstrate that TI-ODE consistently outperforms existing methods and achieves state-of-the-art performance on attribute prediction tasks, and experiments on the \textit{Covid} dataset further verify the interpretability and generalizability of our TI-ODE. Furthermore, we demonstrate both theoretically and empirically that TI-ODE exhibits superior robustness compared to models utilizing a unified message-passing mechanism.

</details>


### [128] [Heterogeneous Variational Inference for Markov Degradation Hazard Models: Discretized Mixture with Interpretable Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24818)
*Takato Yasuno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对贝叶斯有限混合模型在工业设备风险聚类中面临的信号不足、聚类不稳定和MCMC计算不可行三大瓶颈，提出结合8状态全局百分位离散化、30维特征工程、可解释模型选择规则和ADVI全秩协方差估计的实用框架。在280台泵设备上的实验表明：ADVI与NUTS估计结果几乎一致但速度快15倍（随机效应模型），且有限混合模型中ADVI在84倍更短时间内提供稳定结果，无NUTS的收敛问题和标签切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯有限混合模型能够识别工业设备的离散风险簇（如低风险vs高风险），但其实际部署面临三个关键瓶颈：(1) 粗粒度状态离散化导致退化信号不足；(2) 当数据天然支持的簇数少于探索的簇数时，聚类识别不稳定；(3) MCMC方法计算开销巨大（每模型7+小时），无法投入生产环境。

Method: 提出四部分组成的实用框架：(1) 8状态全局百分位离散化，放大退化事件信号；(2) 30维特征工程，整合22个统计趋势特征、连续健康指标和PCA压缩至3维的文本嵌入；(3) 可解释模型选择规则，在WAIC基础上强制执行最小簇份额和簇间分离度约束；(4) 采用全秩协方差ADVI实现稳定快速估计。

Result: 在280台工业泵设备（104,703条检测记录）上：随机效应模型中ADVI与NUTS估计几乎一致，加速15倍；有限混合模型中NUTS存在严重收敛问题和标签切换，而ADVI以84倍更短时间提供稳定结果，并通过可解释性约束确定了最优簇数。

Conclusion: (1) 首次证明细粒度8状态离散化对生存分析中混合模型稳定性的必要性；(2) 提出融合统计、连续和语义信号的综合特征工程策略；(3) 提出防止自动模型选择过拟合的实用可解释性规则；(4) 实证证明ADVI在收敛性、稳定性和计算效率上均优于NUTS。

Abstract: Bayesian finite mixture models can identify discrete risk clusters (low-risk vs. high-risk equipment), but face three critical bottlenecks: (1) insufficient degradation signals from coarse state discretization, (2) unstable cluster identification when data inherently supports fewer clusters than explored, and (3) computational infeasibility of Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods for production deployment (7+ hours per model). We propose a practical framework combining (1) 8-state global percentile discretization that amplifies degradation events, (2) 30-dimensional feature engineering integrating statistical trends (22 features), continuous health indicators, and text embeddings (PCA-compressed to 3 dimensions), (3) interpretable model selection rules enforcing minimum cluster share and separation alongside WAIC, and (4) Automatic Differentiation Variational Inference (ADVI) with full-rank covariance for stable, fast estimation. Applied to 280 industrial pump equipment with 104,703 inspection records, we demonstrate: (1) Random effect models (baseline) show ADVI and NUTS produce nearly identical estimates with 15$\times$ speedup, validating ADVI accuracy. (2) Finite mixture models identify optimal number of clusters with interpretability constraints. (3) NUTS exhibits severe convergence issues and label switching, while ADVI provides stable results in 84$\times$ less time. We contributed that (1) First demonstration that fine-grained state discretization (8-state) is essential for mixture model stability in survival analysis.(2) Comprehensive feature engineering strategy combining statistical, continuous, and semantic signals. (3) Practical interpretability rules preventing overfitting in automated model selection. (4) Empirical evidence that ADVI outperforms NUTS for finite mixture models in terms of convergence, stability, and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [129] [Negative Ontology of True Target for Machine Learning: Towards Evaluation and Learning under Democratic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24824)
*Yongquan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从哲学角度审视了关于“真实目标”（TT）存在性假设的转变如何为基于机器学习的预测建模带来新视角，并基于TT不存在的假设，提出了“民主监督”下的评估与学习知识体系，包括多重不准确真实目标（MIATTs）及其框架EL-MIATTs，并在教育领域进行了应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流机器学习范式默认假设真实目标（TT）客观存在，但这种假设在现实复杂场景中可能不成立。本文旨在通过重新审视TT的存在性假设，从负本体论视角出发，探索在TT不存在的情况下如何重新定义机器学习中的监督与评估机制。

Method: 采用哲学分析的方法，明确采纳“负本体论”立场（即TT在客观世界中不存在）；基于此提出“民主监督”（Democratic Supervision）概念；进一步提出了多重不准确真实目标（MIATTs）作为实例化实现；围绕MIATTs建立了逻辑驱动的生成与评估原则、逻辑评估公式以及不可定义的TT学习机制；最终构建了EL-MIATTs框架。

Result: 提出了EL-MIATTs（Evaluation and Learning with MIATTs）框架，并在真实世界的教育和职业发展应用中展示了该框架的潜力，验证了其能够支持个性化教育与发展。

Conclusion: 通过否定TT的客观存在性，民主监督范式为机器学习提供了一种新的视角和理论框架，EL-MIATTs在实际应用中展现了支持教育与职业发展的潜力，与教育领域的民主监督理念相一致。

Abstract: This article philosophically examines how shifts in assumptions regarding the existence and non-existence of the true target (TT) give rise to new perspectives and insights for machine learning (ML)-based predictive modeling and, correspondingly, proposes a knowledge system for evaluation and learning under Democratic Supervision. By systematically analysing the existence assumption of the TT in current mainstream ML paradigms, we explicitly adopt a negative ontology perspective, positing that the TT does not objectively exist in the real world, and, grounded in this non-existence assumption, define Democratic Supervision for ML. We further present Multiple Inaccurate True Targets (MIATTs) as an instance-level realization of Democratic Supervision. Building upon MIATTs, we derive principles, for the logic-driven generation and assessment of MIATTs, a logical assessment formulation for evaluation with MIATTs, and undefinable true target learning for learning with MIATTs. Based on these components, we establish the evaluation and learning with MIATTs (EL-MIATTs) framework for ML-based predictive modelling. A real-world application demonstrates the potential of the proposed EL-MIATTs framework in supporting education and professional development for individuals, aligning with prior discussions of Democratic Supervision in the fields of education and professional development.

</details>


### [130] [Incompressible Knowledge Probes: Estimating Black-Box LLM Parameter Counts via Factual Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24827)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出不可压缩知识探针（IKP）基准，通过测量模型掌握的事实知识量来下限估计其参数规模，在89个开源模型上实现了R²=0.917的拟合，并发现事实性能力随参数规模对数线性增长，不受推理基准饱和或“致密化定律”的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源前沿模型不公开参数量，而基于推理经济学估算参数量的方法因硬件、批处理和服务栈等外部假设存在2倍以上的不确定性。本文旨在利用模型知识容量与参数量的内在关系，提供更紧致的参数量下限估计方法。

Method: 构建包含1,400个事实性问题的IKP基准，覆盖7个难易等级，设计原则是问题答案不能通过推理推导或由架构改进压缩。在89个开源模型（135M-1,600B参数，19个厂商）上校准IKP准确率与参数量的对数线性映射，使用留一交叉验证评估泛化能力。

Result: 对数线性映射R²=0.917，交叉验证中位数误差1.59倍，68.5%预测在2倍以内，87.6%在3倍以内。MoE模型中总参数比活跃参数更能预测知识量（R²=0.79 vs 0.51）。对188个模型进行评估，给出了主要闭源前沿模型的有效知识容量估计。IKP时间系数为-0.0010/月（不显著），拒绝“致密化定律”预测的+0.0117/月（p<10⁻¹⁵）。

Conclusion: 推理基准的饱和并不代表缩放定律的终结；事实性知识容量继续随参数量对数线性增长，且跨代际、跨厂商保持稳定。安全微调会掩盖模型已知但拒绝回答的知识，导致参数量估计偏低。

Abstract: Closed-source frontier labs do not disclose parameter counts, and the standard alternative -- inference economics -- carries $2\times$+ uncertainty from hardware, batching, and serving-stack assumptions external to the model. We exploit a tighter intrinsic bound: storing $F$ facts requires at least $F/$(bits per parameter) weights, so measuring how much a model \emph{knows} lower-bounds how many parameters it \emph{has}. We introduce \textbf{Incompressible Knowledge Probes (IKPs)}, a benchmark of 1{,}400 factual questions spanning 7 tiers of obscurity, designed to isolate knowledge that cannot be derived by reasoning or compressed by architectural improvements.
  We calibrate a log-linear mapping from IKP accuracy to parameter count on 89 open-weight models (135M--1,600B) spanning 19 vendors, achieving $R^2 = 0.917$; leave-one-out cross-validation confirms generalization (median fold error $1.59\times$, $68.5\%$ within $2\times$ and $87.6\%$ within $3\times$). For Mixture-of-Experts models, total parameters predict knowledge ($R^2 = 0.79$) far better than active parameters ($R^2 = 0.51$). We evaluate 188 models from 27 vendors and estimate effective knowledge capacity for all major proprietary frontier models; for heavily safety-tuned models the estimates are lower bounds, since refusal policy can hide tens of percentage points of "refused but known" capacity.
  The widely-reported saturation of reasoning benchmarks does not imply the end of scaling. Procedural capability compresses under the "Densing Law," but across 96 dated open-weight models the IKP time coefficient is $-0.0010$/month (95\% CI $[-0.0031, +0.0008]$) -- indistinguishable from zero, and rejecting the Densing prediction of $+0.0117$/month at $p < 10^{-15}$. Factual capacity continues to scale log-linearly with parameters across generations and across vendors.

</details>


### [131] [On the Trainability of Masked Diffusion Language Models via Blockwise Locality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24832)
*Yuxiang Wang,Yu Xiang,Baojian Zhou,Qifang Zhao,Keyue Jiang,Yanghua Xiao,Xiaoxiao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了块级掩码扩散语言模型（MDMs）在结构化生成任务中的稳定性问题，发现标准随机掩码MDMs在有序生成任务中存在不稳定性，并提出了两种局部感知块级模型（Jigsaw和Scatter）来注入自回归局部性偏置，从而在不同任务上匹配或优于自回归大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDMs作为自回归语言模型的有前途替代方案，但其优化过程存在显著的不稳定性问题，需要深入理解并加以缓解。

Method: 在三个控制任务（上下文线性回归、图路径寻找、数独求解）上对比块级MDMs与AR-LLMs；提出两种局部感知块级模型Jigsaw和Scatter，通过在块内注入自回归从左到右的归纳偏置，同时在块级别保持迭代细化能力。

Result: 标准随机掩码MDMs在线性回归上不可靠、在图路径寻找上训练方差高，但在数独上优于AR-LLMs；Jigsaw在线性回归上匹配AR-LLM的稳定性并在数独上表现强劲，Scatter在路径寻找上保留了扩散模型的规划优势。

Conclusion: 标准随机掩码MDMs（包括块级变体）对于有序生成任务可能不是最优的扩散LM实现，需要超越随机掩码的模型设计。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to standard autoregressive large language models (AR-LLMs), yet their optimization can be substantially less stable. We study blockwise MDMs and compare them with AR-LLMs on three controlled tasks that stress different aspects of structured generation: in-context linear regression, graph path-finding, and Sudoku solving. We find that standard random-masking MDMs fail to reliably learn linear regression, exhibit high variance training dynamics on graph path-finding, while outperforming AR-LLMs on Sudoku. To mitigate these instabilities, we propose two locality aware blockwise models, namely Jigsaw and Scatter, that inject left-to-right inductive bias by enforcing autoregressive locality within blocks while preserving iterative refinement at the block level. Empirically, Jigsaw matches AR-LLM stability on linear regression and remains strong on Sudoku, while Scatter retains diffusion's planning advantage on path-finding. Our results indicate that standard random-masking MDMs, even with blockwise variants, may be a suboptimal instantiation of diffusion LMs for ordered generation, motivating models beyond random masking.

</details>


### [132] [Generative diffusion models for spatiotemporal influenza forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24913)
*Joseph Lemaitre,Justin Lessler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Influpaint将去噪扩散概率模型应用于流感疫情预测，通过将流感季节编码为时空图像（像素强度表示发病率），利用监测与模拟混合数据学习疾病动力学分布，将预测视为条件生成（图像修复）任务。在回顾性评估中达到领先集成方法水平的预测精度，在2023-2025年美国CDC FluSight挑战中表现优异，最佳训练数据配比为30%监测+70%模拟轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传染病预测对公共卫生规划至关重要，但现有机制模型和统计方法难以捕捉多模态不确定性及新兴趋势，亟需能够表征复杂流行病动力学不确定性的灵活框架。

Method: 采用去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM），将流感季节数据编码为时空图像（像素强度=发病率），使用监测数据与模拟轨迹的混合数据集进行训练，将预测任务转化为条件生成（图像修复/inpainting）任务，从部分观测中生成完整轨迹。

Result: 生成真实且多样化的流行病轨迹；回顾性评估中准确性与领先集成方法持平；2023-2025年实时评估中性能显著提升，2024-2025年预测高度准确但略有过自信；最佳训练数据配比为30%监测+70%模拟。

Conclusion: 扩散模型能够捕捉流感动力学的关键时空结构，为概率性传染病预测提供了一种灵活且有效的框架，证明了生成式AI在流行病学预测中的潜力。

Abstract: Forecasting infectious disease incidence can provide important information to guide public health planning, yet is difficult because epidemic dynamics are complex. Current mechanistic and statistical approaches often struggle to capture multimodal uncertainty or emergent trends. Influpaint adapts denoising diffusion probabilistic models to epidemic forecasting. By encoding influenza seasons as spatiotemporal images in which pixel intensity represents incidence, Influpaint learns a rich distribution of disease dynamics from a hybrid dataset of surveillance and simulated trajectories. Forecasting is formulated as a conditional generation (inpainting) task from partial observations. We show that Influpaint generates realistic, diverse epidemic trajectories and achieves forecast accuracy that is competitive with leading ensemble methods in retrospective evaluation. In real-time evaluation during the 2023--2025 U.S. CDC FluSight challenges, performance improved substantially across seasons, with highly accurate but somewhat overconfident projections in 2024--2025. The best performance was achieved with a training dataset containing 30% surveillance and 70% simulated trajectories. These results show that diffusion models can capture important spatiotemporal structure in influenza dynamics and provide a flexible framework for probabilistic infectious disease forecasting.

</details>


### [133] [A Unifying Framework for Unsupervised Concept Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24936)
*Chandler Squires,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的理论框架，将无监督概念提取任务视为识别生成模型，并给出一个通用元定理来简化可识别性保证的证明过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当提取的概念用于模型引导（steering）和遗忘（unlearning）等下游任务时，需要理解其保证（或缺乏保证）。现有方法缺乏统一的理论框架来建立可识别性保证。

Method: 将概念提取任务形式化为识别生成模型，提出一个通用元定理，将建立可识别性保证的问题简化为刻画两个集合的交集问题。

Result: 该元定理在多种广泛使用的方法上得到验证，显著简化了证明可识别性保证的任务。

Conclusion: 为开发新的、有原则的概念提取方法铺平了道路。

Abstract: Techniques for concept extraction, such as sparse autoencoders and transcoders, aim to extract high-level symbolic concepts from low-level nonsymbolic representations. When these extracted concepts are used for downstream tasks such as model steering and unlearning, it is essential to understand their guarantees, or lack thereof. In this work, we present a unified theoretical framework for unsupervised concept extraction, in which we frame the task of concept extraction as identifying a generative model. We present a general meta-theorem for identifiability, which reduces the problem of establishing identifiability guarantees to the problem of characterizing the intersection of two sets. As we demonstrate on a range of widely-used approaches, this meta-theorem substantially simplifies the task of proving such guarantees, thus paving the way for the development of new, principled approaches for concept extraction.

</details>


### [134] [Compute Aligned Training: Optimizing for Test Time Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24957)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出计算对齐训练（Compute Aligned Training）方法，将训练目标与测试时计算策略对齐，通过将推理策略视为基策略上的算子推导新损失函数，实证表明该方法能显著提升测试时扩展效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准后训练范式（SFT和RL）优化单个样本在基策略下的似然，这与依赖聚合或过滤输出的测试时计算策略存在错配，导致训练与测试时目标不一致。

Method: 提出计算对齐训练，将测试时推理策略概念化为基策略上的算子，据此推导新的损失函数，并在SFT和RL框架下针对常见测试时策略进行实例化。

Result: 实证结果表明，相比标准训练，所提训练方法能显著改善测试时计算扩展的效果。

Conclusion: 计算对齐训练有效弥合了训练目标与测试时策略之间的差距，为提升LLM在测试时计算扩展场景下的性能提供了新范式。

Abstract: Scaling test-time compute has emerged as a powerful mechanism for enhancing Large Language Model (LLM) performance. However, standard post-training paradigms, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL), optimize the likelihood of individual samples under a base policy, creating a misalignment with test time procedures that rely on aggregated or filtered outputs. In this work, we propose Compute Aligned Training, which aligns training objectives with test-time strategies. By conceptualizing inference strategies as operators on the base policy, we derive new loss functions that maximize performance when said strategies are applied. We instantiate such loss functions for SFT and RL across common test time strategies. Finally, we provide empirical evidence that this training method substantially improves test time scaling over standard training.

</details>


### [135] [CoreFlow: Low-Rank Matrix Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24959)
*Dongze Wu,Linglingzhi Zhu,Yao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoreFlow，一种几何保持的低秩流模型，通过在共享行/列子空间上训练连续归一化流来生成矩阵值分布，在高维小样本场景下显著提升生成质量和训练效率，且能处理不完整训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维且可能不完整的矩阵值数据在训练样本有限时，环境空间生成建模计算昂贵且统计脆弱，亟需一种能利用共享低秩几何结构的高效生成模型。

Method: 先学习跨矩阵分布的共享行/列子空间，将矩阵投影到诱导的低维核心上，然后仅在该核心上训练连续归一化流（CNF）。通过掩码黎曼更新和迭代补全处理不完整训练矩阵。

Result: 在真实和合成基准上，小样本场景下谱级和矩级生成质量显著提升；数据充足时仍具竞争力。可压缩至环境维度的9%，并容忍高达40%的缺失训练条目。

Conclusion: CoreFlow通过分离共享矩阵几何与样本特异性变化，在保持矩阵结构的同时大幅提升训练效率，为高维有限样本矩阵生成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Learning matrix-valued distributions from high-dimensional and possibly incomplete training data is challenging: ambient-space generative modeling is computationally expensive and statistically fragile when the matrix dimension is large but the sample size is limited. We propose CoreFlow, a geometry-preserving low-rank flow model that learns shared row/column subspaces across the matrix distribution, and then trains a continuous normalizing flow only on the induced low-dimensional core. CoreFlow is designed for settings where shared low-rank matrix geometry is present, especially in high-dimensional limited-sample regimes. This separates shared matrix geometry from sample-specific variation, preserves matrix structure, and substantially improves training efficiency. The same framework also handles incomplete training matrices through masked Riemannian updates and iterative completion. Across real and synthetic benchmarks, CoreFlow substantially improves spectral and moment-level generation quality in few-sample regimes while remaining competitive in data-rich settings, even under compression to 9% of the ambient dimension and with up to 40% missing training entries.

</details>


### [136] [Odysseys: Benchmarking Web Agents on Realistic Long Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24964)
*Lawrence Keunho Jang,Jing Yu Koh,Daniel Fried,Ruslan Salakhutdinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Odysseys基准，包含200个基于真实浏览会话的长时跨域Web任务，使用基于评分标准的细粒度评估（平均6.1个分级标准），并引入轨迹效率指标。最强模型成功率为44.5%，轨迹效率仅1.15%，表明长时Web智能体仍有巨大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web智能体基准大多聚焦于短时单站点任务，前沿模型已接近饱和。然而现实Web使用涉及长时、多站点的复杂工作流，现有基准无法有效评估此类能力。

Method: 从真实浏览会话中提取200个长时Web任务；采用基于评分标准的评估方式（每任务平均6.1个分级标准），替代传统的二元通过/失败评估；引入轨迹效率指标（评分/步骤数）衡量智能体的效率。

Result: 最强模型成功率为44.5%，轨迹效率仅1.15%；基于评分标准的评估与人类一致性更高，比LLM-as-a-judge的轨迹级评估提供更细致的信号。

Conclusion: 长时跨域Web智能体当前性能严重不足，效率是首要关注指标；Odysseys为衡量真实计算机使用智能体提供了现实的基准。

Abstract: Existing web agent benchmarks have largely converged on short, single-site tasks that frontier models are approaching saturation on. However, real world web use consists of long-horizon, multi-site workflows. Common web navigation tasks, such as comparing products across different domains, planning trips across multiple services, or summarizing information from multiple search queries, require sustained context and cross-site reasoning over potentially hours of browsing. To capture and evaluate such behaviors, we introduce Odysseys: a benchmark of 200 long-horizon web tasks derived from real world browsing sessions evaluated on the live Internet. We find that binary pass/fail evaluation is inadequate for long-horizon settings and introduce a rubric-based evaluation, annotating each Odysseys task with an average of 6.1 graded rubrics. We demonstrate that this yields higher agreement with humans and provides a more fine-grained signal than commonly used trajectory-level LLM-as-a-judge evaluation metrics. We tested several leading frontier models and find that the strongest models achieve a success rate of 44.5%, which leaves substantial room for future improvements. Beyond task success, we argue that efficiency is a first-class concern for long-horizon agents. We introduce a Trajectory Efficiency metric (rubric score per step) and find that even frontier agents achieve only 1.15%, marking an evident need for agents that can succeed efficiently and not simply eventually. Odysseys isolates the critical evaluation of long-horizon proficiency in open-web environments, providing a realistic benchmark to measure progress towards computer-use agents that can potentially productively operate for hours. We release our tasks, evaluation scripts, and other results at https://odysseys-website.pages.dev

</details>


### [137] [Null Measurability at the Symmetrization Interface in VC Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25028)
*Dhruv Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在统计学习基本定理中，仅需比Borel可测性更弱的解析可测性条件即可支撑对称化论证，并构造了严格分离的例子，同时在Lean 4中完成了形式化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对称化证明中使用的单侧ghost-gap上确界所需的可测性条件比Borel可测性更弱，但现有理论对此要求过强，需厘清实际所需的最小可测性假设。

Method: 利用解析集理论和Choquet容量定理证明坏事件的可测性；构造反例实现严格分离；证明该弱正则性在若干概念类构造子下封闭；在Lean 4中形式化主要结论。

Result: (1) 对Polish空间上Borel参数化的概念类，坏事件是解析集，因此在任意有限Borel测度的完备化下可测；(2) 存在概念类其坏事件是零测可测但非Borel可测；(3) 该弱正则性在拼接、固定及可数插值、纤维积复合下封闭；(4) 在可实现设定下，削弱了从有限VC维到PAC可学习性所需的可测性假设。

Conclusion: 对称化证明实际所需的可测性条件弱于Borel可测性，解析可测性足以支撑，且该弱正则性具有良好的封闭性，这为统计学习理论提供了更精细的可测性基础，并在Lean 4中完成了形式化验证。

Abstract: Recent work revisiting measurability in the fundamental theorem of statistical learning imposes Borel measurability of ghost-gap suprema. We show that, at the one-sided ghost-gap interface actually used by the standard symmetrization proof, this requirement is stronger than necessary. For any Borel-parameterized concept class on a Polish domain, the bad event "there exists a hypothesis whose ghost empirical error exceeds its training empirical error by at least ε/2" is analytic. By Choquet capacitability, it is therefore measurable in the completion of every finite Borel measure. We then construct a concept class whose bad event is null-measurable but not Borel, giving a strict separation from the Borel supremum condition. Finally, we prove closure under patching, fixed and countable interpolation, and fiber-product amalgamation, showing that the weaker regularity level is stable under natural concept-class constructors. In the realizable setting, where targets belong to the class and are measurable, these results weaken the measurability hypothesis needed by the symmetrization route from finite VC dimension to PAC learnability. The main results and the descriptive-set-theoretic infrastructure used by them are formalized in Lean 4.

</details>


### [138] [PolyKV: A Shared Asymmetrically-Compressed KV Cache Pool for Multi-Agent LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24971)
*Ishan Patel,Ishan Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyKV提出了一种多推理智能体共享单一非对称压缩KV缓存池的系统，相比传统每智能体独立KV缓存方案，在Llama-3-8B上15个智能体共享4K上下文时，KV缓存内存从19.8GB降至0.45GB（减少97.7%），压缩比稳定在2.91x，困惑度仅增加0.57%，BERTScore F1为0.928。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体并发推理场景中，每个智能体都独立维护各自的KV缓存，导致内存开销随智能体数量线性增长，缺乏高效的缓存共享机制。

Method: PolyKV采用非对称压缩策略：Key以int8 (q8_0)量化以保持softmax稳定性，Value通过TurboQuant MSE方法压缩——先进行快速沃尔什-哈达玛变换（FWHT）旋转，再使用3-bit Lloyd-Max量化（质心调谐至N(0,1)）。单一压缩缓存通过HuggingFace DynamicCache对象注入到N个独立智能体上下文中。

Result: 在SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct和Llama-3-8B-Instruct两个模型、600-7194 token三种上下文长度、最多15个并发智能体上评估。Llama-3-8B+15智能体+4K上下文下内存减少97.7%，PPL仅增0.57%，BERTScore F1为0.928。PPL增量不随智能体数增长，且在1851连贯token上下文中PPL反转为-0.26%（优于基线）。

Conclusion: PolyKV是首个将单一共享有损压缩KV池与多读并发智能体访问相结合的工作，在显著降低内存开销的同时保持了生成质量，且压缩性能随上下文长度增加而改善。

Abstract: We present PolyKV, a system in which multiple concurrent inference agents share a single, asymmetrically compressed KV cache pool. Rather than allocating a separate KV cache per agent -- the standard paradigm -- PolyKV writes a compressed cache once and injects it into N independent agent contexts via HuggingFace DynamicCache objects. Compression is asymmetric: Keys are quantized at int8 (q8_0) to preserve softmax stability, while Values are compressed using TurboQuant MSE -- a Fast Walsh-Hadamard Transform (FWHT) rotation followed by 3-bit Lloyd-Max quantization with centroids tuned to N(0,1). We evaluate across two model scales (SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct and Llama-3-8B-Instruct), three context lengths (600-7,194 tokens), and up to 15 concurrent agents. PolyKV achieves a stable 2.91x compression ratio across all configurations. On Llama-3-8B with 15 agents sharing a 4K-token context, PolyKV reduces KV cache memory from 19.8 GB to 0.45 GB -- a 97.7% reduction -- while maintaining only +0.57% perplexity degradation and a mean BERTScore F1 of 0.928. PPL delta does not grow with agent count and improves as context length increases, inverting to -0.26% at 1,851 coherent tokens. To our knowledge, no prior work combines a single shared, lossy-compressed KV pool with multi-reader concurrent agent access.

</details>


### [139] [VLM Judges Can Rank but Cannot Score: Task-Dependent Uncertainty in Multimodal Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25235)
*Divake Kumar,Sina Tayebati,Devashri Naik,Ranganath Krishnan,Amit Ranjan Trivedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了将共形预测应用于VLM-as-a-Judge的可靠性评估，发现评估不确定性高度依赖任务类型（覆盖范围从40%到70%），并识别出"排序-评分解耦"这一失败模式，同时证明区间宽度主要受任务难度和标注质量驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）越来越多地被用作多模态系统的自动评判器，但其输出的分数缺乏可靠性指示。现有方法无法提供校准过的置信区间，亟需一种无需重新训练、分布无关的框架来量化评估不确定性。

Method: 采用共形预测（conformal prediction）这一分布无关框架，仅利用评分令牌的对数概率（score-token log-probabilities）将评判器的点分数转换为校准后的预测区间，无需重新训练模型。在3个不同评判器和14个视觉任务类别上进行了系统性分析。

Result: 评估不确定性高度依赖任务类型：美感和自然图像的区间覆盖约40%的分数范围，而图表和数学推理任务扩展到约70%。发现"排序-评分解耦"（ranking-scoring decoupling）失败模式，即评判器排序相关性高但区间过宽且无信息量。区间宽度主要由任务难度和标注质量驱动，在清洁的多标注者字幕基准上区间宽度缩小4.5倍。

Conclusion: 共形预测能为VLM评判器提供校准的不确定性量化，揭示了任务依赖的可靠性图谱，同时指出了传统评估指标无法捕捉的排序-评分解耦问题。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as automated judges for multimodal systems, yet their scores provide no indication of reliability. We study this problem through conformal prediction, a distribution-free framework that converts a judge's point score into a calibrated prediction interval using only score-token log-probabilities, with no retraining. We present the first systematic analysis of conformal prediction for VLM-as-a-Judge across 3 judges and 14 visual task categories. Our results show that evaluation uncertainty is strongly task-dependent: intervals cover ~40% of the score range for aesthetics and natural images but expand to ~70% for chart and mathematical reasoning, yielding a quantitative reliability map for multimodal evaluation. We further identify a failure mode not captured by standard evaluation metrics, ranking-scoring decoupling, where judges achieve high ranking correlation while producing wide, uninformative intervals, correctly ordering responses but failing to assign reliable absolute scores. Finally, we show that interval width is driven primarily by task difficulty and annotation quality, i.e., the same judge and method yield 4.5x narrower intervals on a clean, multi-annotator captioning benchmark. Code: https://github.com/divake/VLM-Judge-Uncertainty

</details>


### [140] [Laplace-Bridged Randomized Smoothing for Fast Certified Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24993)
*Miao Lin,MD Saifur Rahman Mazumder,Feng Yu,Daniel Takabi,Rui Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出拉普拉斯桥平滑（LBS），通过将高维输入空间的蒙特卡洛采样替换为低维概率空间的解析计算，在无需噪声增强训练的情况下实现高效认证鲁棒性，并在边缘设备上获得最高494倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机平滑（RS）存在两个实际瓶颈：(i) 依赖噪声增强训练，增加训练成本、降低干净精度，削弱其作为事后防御的性质；(ii) 认证计算昂贵，每样本需数万次前向传播，难以部署在资源受限的边缘设备。

Method: 提出拉普拉斯桥平滑（LBS），将高维输入空间的蒙特卡洛采样替换为低维概率空间的解析计算，实现无需噪声增强训练的高效认证。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上，LBS获得比RS更强的认证鲁棒性，每样本认证成本降低近一个数量级；在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano和Raspberry Pi 4上实现高达494倍加速。

Conclusion: LBS通过解析公式替代蒙特卡洛采样，保留了形式化鲁棒性保证，同时消除了噪声增强训练需求并大幅降低认证开销，使认证部署在实际边缘设备上成为可能。

Abstract: Randomized Smoothing (RS) offers formal $\ell_2$ guarantees for arbitrary base classifiers but faces two key practical bottlenecks: (i) it often relies on noise-augmented training to achieve nontrivial certificates, which increases training cost, can reduce clean accuracy, and weakens RS as a genuinely post-hoc defense; and (ii) certification is computationally expensive, typically requiring tens of thousands of noisy forward passes per input, which hinders deployment, especially on resource-constrained edge devices. To address both limitations, we propose Laplace-Bridged Smoothing (LBS), an analytic reformulation of RS that replaces high-dimensional input-space Monte Carlo (MC) sampling with efficient computations in a low-dimensional probability space. LBS preserves formal robustness guarantees without requiring noise-augmented training while substantially reducing certification burden. On CIFAR-10 and ImageNet, LBS attains stronger certified robustness than RS and reduces per-sample certification cost by nearly an order of magnitude. Notably, on NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano and Raspberry Pi 4, LBS achieves speedups of up to $494\times$, enabling practical certified deployment on real-world edge devices. Finally, we provide theoretical justification for the analytic formulation and certificate validity of LBS.

</details>


### [141] [Online combinatorial optimization with stochastic decision sets and adversarial losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25269)
*Gergely Neu,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对带有随机可用性的不可靠组合动作的序列学习问题，提出了基于Follow-The-Perturbed-Leader的算法，并设计了名为Counting Asleep Times的新型损失估计技术，在完整信息、（半）bandit以及受限信息三种反馈设置下均给出了遗憾界，显著改进了随机可用性下的睡觉bandit问题的最佳已知性能保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列学习研究大多假设动作集固定且始终可用，但在实际应用中（如传感器读数子集选择、道路段选择、库存商品选择等），动作常因设备故障、拥堵或缺货而随机不可用，现有方法无法有效处理这类不可靠组合动作的随机可用性问题。

Method: 基于Follow-The-Perturbed-Leader预测方法，提出名为Counting Asleep Times的新型损失估计技术，针对完整信息、（半）bandit及受限信息三种反馈设置分别设计算法。

Result: 在三种信息反馈设置下均给出了相应的遗憾界理论保证；在随机可用性的睡觉bandit问题上，显著改进了已知高效算法的最佳性能保证；实验验证了所提算法优于已有方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法能够有效处理带有随机可用性的不可靠组合动作的序列学习问题，在理论上提供了更优的遗憾界保证，在实验上展现了优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Most work on sequential learning assumes a fixed set of actions that are available all the time. However, in practice, actions can consist of picking subsets of readings from sensors that may break from time to time, road segments that can be blocked or goods that are out of stock. In this paper we study learning algorithms that are able to deal with stochastic availability of such unreliable composite actions. We propose and analyze algorithms based on the Follow-The-Perturbed-Leader prediction method for several learning settings differing in the feedback provided to the learner. Our algorithms rely on a novel loss estimation technique that we call Counting Asleep Times. We deliver regret bounds for our algorithms for the previously studied full information and (semi-)bandit settings, as well as a natural middle point between the two that we call the restricted information setting. A special consequence of our results is a significant improvement of the best known performance guarantees achieved by an efficient algorithm for the sleeping bandit problem with stochastic availability. Finally, we evaluate our algorithms empirically and show their improvement over the known approaches.

</details>


### [142] [Why Search When You Can Transfer? Amortized Agentic Workflow Design from Structural Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25012)
*Shiyi Du,Jiayuan Liu,Weihua Du,Yue Huang,Jiayi Li,Yingtao Luo,Xiangliang Zhang,Vincent Conitzer,Carl Kingsford*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SWIFT提出了一种通过少样本迁移将工作流设计摊销为可复用的结构先验的方法，完全绕过迭代搜索。在五个基准上超越最先进的基于搜索的方法，同时将边际优化成本降低三个数量级，并验证了迁移的主要是拓扑结构而非表面语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的自动化智能体工作流设计依赖逐任务的迭代搜索，计算成本高昂且无法跨任务复用结构知识。作者观察到优化后的工作流收敛到少量领域特定的拓扑结构，表明组合搜索在很大程度上是冗余的。

Method: SWIFT首先从源任务的搜索轨迹中通过对比分析提取组合启发式规则和输出接口契约；推理时，将先验知识与跨任务工作流演示一起输入单个LLM生成过程，直接合成完整可执行的工作流，完全绕过迭代搜索。

Result: 在五个基准上超越最先进的搜索方法，边际优化成本降低三个数量级；泛化到四个未见过的基准；从GPT-4o-mini成功迁移到Grok、Qwen、Gemma三个基础模型。替换操作名为随机字符串后仍保留93%以上的平均性能。

Conclusion: 工作流演示迁移的主要是拓扑结构而非表面语义，证明了结构先验的可复用性，为高效零迭代工作流设计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Automated agentic workflow design currently relies on per-task iterative search, which is computationally prohibitive and fails to reuse structural knowledge across tasks. We observe that optimized workflows converge to a small family of domain-specific topologies, suggesting that this combinatorial search is largely redundant. Building on this insight, we propose SWIFT (Synthesizing Workflows via Few-shot Transfer), a framework that amortizes workflow design into reusable structural priors. SWIFT first distills compositional heuristics and output-interface contracts from contrastive analysis of prior search trajectories across source tasks. At inference time, it conditions a single LLM generation pass on these priors together with cross-task workflow demonstrations to synthesize a complete, executable workflow for an unseen target task, bypassing iterative search entirely. On five benchmarks, SWIFT outperforms the state-of-the-art search-based method while reducing marginal per-task optimization cost by three orders of magnitude. It further generalizes to four additional unseen benchmarks and transfers successfully from GPT-4o-mini to three additional foundation models (Grok, Qwen, Gemma). Controlled ablations reveal that workflow demonstrations primarily transfer topological structure rather than surface semantics: replacing all operator names with random strings still retains over 93% of the full system's average performance.

</details>


### [143] [Dynamic Regret for Online Regression in RKHS via Discounted VAW and Subspace Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25021)
*Dmitry B. Rokhlin,Georgiy A. Karapetyants*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中平方损失下的在线回归动态遗憾问题。通过有限维子空间近似将折扣Vovk-Azoury-Warmuth方法推广到RKHS设置，并引入正交截断方法，针对高斯核、解析点积核、Matérn核等不同核函数分别给出了快速与慢速收敛的动态遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有限维情形下基于折扣VAW方法的动态遗憾界推广到RKHS这种无限维非平稳在线学习场景，以处理时间变化的比较器序列。

Method: 1) 利用有限维子空间近似将Jacobsen & Cutkosky的折扣VAW方法迁移到RKHS；2) 在固定子空间上运行基于VAW的折扣VAW预报器集成（几何网格折扣因子）；3) 提出正交截断方法：通过构造特征函数的正交基，取前k个基函数张成有限维近似空间；4) 针对不同核（高斯核、解析点积核通过显式特征展开，Mercer截断通过谱近似，Matérn核通过核截面张成的子空间）分别应用该框架。

Result: 显式特征展开为高斯核和解析点积核提供了快速收敛的动态遗憾界；Mercer截断根据特征值衰减速率分别给出了快速和慢速两种模态的界；核截面构造方法应用于Matérn核也获得了相应的动态遗憾界。

Conclusion: 提出的正交截断框架能有效将有限维动态遗憾方法扩展到RKHS，并且根据核函数的不同谱特性（特征值衰减快慢）可以自适应地得到快速或慢速收敛的动态遗憾界。

Abstract: We study online regression with the square loss in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space under a dynamic regret criterion. The learner is compared with a time-varying comparator sequence, and the bounds depend on its path length in the RKHS norm. The proposed method transfers the finite-dimensional discounted Vovk--Azoury--Warmuth approach of Jacobsen \& Cutkosky (2024) to the RKHS setting by means of finite-dimensional subspace approximations. For a fixed subspace, we run a VAW-based ensemble of discounted VAW forecasters over a geometric grid of discount factors. The additional approximation error is controlled by the uniform projection error of kernel sections.
  We then introduce a general orthogonal truncation method: starting from a feature expansion of the kernel, we construct the associated RKHS by introducing an inner product that makes the feature functions orthonormal, and then use the spans of the first basis functions as finite-dimensional approximation spaces. The resulting subspace reduction is applied to several approximation schemes. Explicit feature expansions yield fast-regime bounds for Gaussian and analytic dot-product kernels. Mercer truncations provide a spectral approximation method and lead to dynamic regret bounds in fast and slow regimes, depending on the eigenvalue decay. Finally, we study subspaces spanned by kernel sections and apply this construction to Matérn kernels.

</details>


### [144] [CiteRadar: A Citation Intelligence Platform for Researcher Profiling and Geographic Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25057)
*Chenxu Niu,Yiming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CiteRadar是一个开源系统，只需输入Google Scholar用户ID，即可自动生成包含完整论文列表、引文元数据、作者排名表、统计摘要和交互式世界地图的结构化输出文件夹。该系统集成了五个异构数据源，通过五阶段管道处理，并解决了Scholar元字符串解析、作者消歧、OpenAlex URL转换等关键技术问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献计量平台要么需要昂贵的机构订阅，要么只提供聚合的被引计数，缺乏细粒度的逐作者元数据。学者们缺乏便捷、免费的工具来了解自己引文的地理覆盖范围和学术社区结构，而这些信息对职业发展、基金申请和合作发现越来越重要。

Method: CiteRadar采用五阶段流水线架构，集成Google Scholar、OpenAlex、CrossRef、Semantic Scholar和OpenStreetMap Nominatim五个异构数据源。关键技术贡献包括：(1) Scholar元字符串解析器，能处理Unicode非断空格分隔符；(2) 两阶段作者消歧系统，使用停用词过滤后的机构名相似度防止同名实体合并错误；(3) OpenAlex网页URL到API URL的转换修复，将带城市级位置数据的作者记录比例从0%提升至约60%；(4) 对数刻度的交互式Folium世界地图，包含每城市研究者弹窗。

Result: 该工具通过单次命令行调用即可自动生成：完整论文列表、所有引文及增强的作者元数据、两个排名作者表（按被引频率和h指数）、纯文本统计摘要、以及自包含的交互式HTML世界地图。作者消歧系统可消除高达9倍正确值的h指数归属误差；OpenAlex URL修复将城市级位置数据覆盖率从0%提升至约60%。

Conclusion: CiteRadar填补了学术影响力地理可视化和细粒度引文分析的可访问工具空白，通过开源方式为学者提供了免费、自动化的引文地理分布分析方案，具有重要的实用价值和可复现性。

Abstract: Understanding the geographic reach and community structure of one's scholarly citations is increasingly valuable for career development, grant applications, and collaboration discovery -- yet accessible tools for answering these questions remain scarce. Existing bibliometric platforms either require costly institutional subscriptions or expose only aggregate citation counts without granular per-author metadata. We present CiteRadar, an open-source system that accepts a single Google Scholar user identifier and automatically produces a structured output folder containing: the author's complete publication list, all retrieved citing papers with enriched author metadata, two ranked author tables (by citation frequency and by h-index), a plain-text statistical summary, and a self-contained interactive HTML world map -- all from a single command-line invocation. CiteRadar integrates five heterogeneous data sources -- Google Scholar, OpenAlex, CrossRef, Semantic Scholar, and OpenStreetMap Nominatim -- through a carefully engineered five-stage pipeline. Key technical contributions include: (1) a Scholar meta-string parser resilient to Unicode non-breaking-space separators, a pervasive but undocumented quirk in Scholar's HTML that silently corrupts venue and year fields when unhandled; (2) a two-stage author disambiguation system using stop-word-filtered institution name similarity to guard against the well-known same-name entity-merging failure mode in bibliometric databases, demonstrated to eliminate h-index attribution errors of up to 9x the correct value; (3) an OpenAlex web-URL to API-URL conversion fix that raises the fraction of author records with city-level location data from 0% to ~60%; and (4) a logarithmically-scaled interactive Folium world map with per-city researcher popups, rendered as a fully self-contained HTML file.

</details>


### [145] [Feasible-First Exploration for Constrained ML Deployment Optimization in Crash-Prone Hierarchical Search Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25073)
*Christian Lysenstøen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对机器学习模型部署中存在大量无效配置的恶劣搜索空间，提出Thermal Budget Annealing (TBA)方法，通过可行优先探索+热启动TPE，并引入超时终止和子空间黑名单机制，在有限评估预算下有效发现可行且高性能的部署配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实生产环境部署机器学习模型时，需要联合优化模型家族、量化方案、运行时后端和服务配置，但搜索空间中存在大量无效配置（崩溃、超内存、延迟违规），标准黑箱优化器在无效配置多的恶劣空间下会将大量预算浪费在无效试验上。

Method: 提出Thermal Budget Annealing (TBA)，一种可行优先探索流程：先映射有效可行区域，再用其热启动TPE。包含两个鲁棒性机制：1）超时终止，提前中止明显不可行的评估；2）子空间黑名单，在连续失败后暂时抑制分类子空间。

Result: 在合成基准和5个预训练视觉模型×5种GPU目标（H100、A100、RTX 5080、L4、T4）的真实部署实验中，TBA在严格预算下提升了模型家族的发现能力，相比冷启动TPE减少了预算浪费。

Conclusion: 将优化分解为显式探索阶段和模型引导的利用阶段，结合超时和子空间黑名单等鲁棒机制，能有效应对恶劣部署空间中大量无效配置带来的挑战。

Abstract: Deploying machine learning models under production constraints requires joint optimization over model family, quantization scheme, runtime backend, and serving configuration. This induces a hierarchical mixed-variable search space in which many configurations are invalid: evaluations may crash, exceed memory limits, or violate latency constraints. Standard black-box optimizers such as Tree-structured Parzen Estimators (TPE) and constrained Bayesian optimization are effective when valid configurations are common, but they can spend a large fraction of a small evaluation budget on invalid or uninformative trials in hostile deployment spaces. This paper studies that regime and asks whether optimization should be decomposed into an explicit exploration stage followed by model-guided exploitation. We propose Thermal Budget Annealing (TBA), a feasible-first exploration procedure that maps valid and feasible regions before warm-starting TPE. The method includes two robustness mechanisms for hostile hardware: trial timeouts that abort clearly infeasible evaluations early, and subspace blacklisting that temporarily suppresses categorical subspaces after repeated failures. We also introduce DeployBench, a benchmark suite for deployment optimization with hierarchical structure, hidden crash zones, hard constraints, and unequal evaluation costs. On synthetic benchmarks and real GPU deployment with five pre-trained vision models across five GPU targets (NVIDIA H100, A100, RTX 5080, L4, and T4), the proposed hybrid improves model-family discovery under tight constraints while reducing wasted budget relative to cold-start TPE.

</details>
